Telecommunications experts from major global operators, manufacturers, government […] <p>Samsung Electronics today announced that it is bringing together global telecommunications industry partners at the Samsung Research America campus in Mountain View, California for the Silicon Valley Future Wireless Summit 2026 on Oct. 15 (local time), following Partner Day held at the same venue the prior day.</p>



<p>Telecommunications experts from major global operators, manufacturers, government agencies, and academia are participating in the events, with more than 100 attendees gathering to exchange insights and explore the future of next-generation networks.</p>



<figure class="wp-block-image size-full"><img loading="lazy" width="1000" height="723" src="https://img.global.news.samsung.com/global/wp-content/uploads/2026/10/15072650/Samsung-Corporate-Silicon-Valley-Future-Wireless-Summit-2026_main1F.jpg" alt="A view of Partner Day in Mountain View, California, on Oct 14" class="wp-image-177764" /><figcaption class="wp-element-caption">▲ A view of Partner Day in Mountain View, California, on Oct 14</figcaption></figure>



<p>Samsung is discussing the evolution of software-driven networks for the AI and 6G eras, as well as ecosystem-wide collaboration that creates new value across the telecommunications industry. The company is also showcasing its leadership in 6G communication technology, sharing achievements in AI-native technologies applied to its communication systems, and providing other updates.</p>



<p></p>



<h2 class="wp-block-heading"><strong>Collaboration To Create New Network Value in the AI Era</strong></h2>



<p>Partner Day fostered engaging conversations grounded in the expertise and experience of leaders from across the industry. Participants examined software-driven network strategies and ecosystem-wide collaboration as the convergence of AI and networks accelerates the evolution of network infrastructure.</p>



<p>Samsung opened the event with a keynote highlighting the way AI is transforming the architectural foundation that has defined telecommunications networks for more than a century. The company emphasized that, moving beyond the limitations of existing technologies, the convergence of connectivity and AI will be a key driver of new ways of communication that humanity has long imagined. Drawing on the industry’s collective intelligence, Samsung then delved into the strategic direction of network evolution in the AI era.</p>



<figure class="wp-block-image size-full"><img loading="lazy" width="1000" height="906" src="https://img.global.news.samsung.com/global/wp-content/uploads/2026/10/15072735/Samsung-Corporate-Silicon-Valley-Future-Wireless-Summit-2026_main2F.jpg" alt="Woojune Kim, President and Head of Networks Business at Samsung Electronics, is giving his keynote speech at Partner Day, on Oct 14" class="wp-image-177765" /><figcaption class="wp-element-caption">▲ Woojune Kim, President and Head of Networks Business at Samsung Electronics, is giving his keynote speech at Partner Day, on Oct 14.</figcaption></figure>



<p>The panel sessions centered on experiences and key lessons learned from the commercialization of virtualized networks, exploring how the ecosystem can continue to evolve. Participants also discussed ways to move beyond connectivity by developing new services and translating them into tangible business opportunities.</p>



<p>“Networks in the AI era are evolving into intelligent infrastructure that can understand the diverse requirements of a growing range of new services, flexibly adapt through virtualization, and deliver optimal solutions for each need,” said Angelo Jeongho Park, Executive Vice President and Head of Global Strategic Sales, Networks Business at Samsung Electronics. “Together with our industry-leading partners, Samsung has successfully commercialized virtualized networks at scale. By adding advanced AI technologies to that foundation, we will transform communications infrastructure into a new engine for growth and lead the way toward the industry’s next leap forward.”</p>



<p></p>



<h2 class="wp-block-heading"><strong>AI-Driven Innovation in Wireless Communications</strong></h2>



<p>The Silicon Valley Future Wireless Summit is opening with keynote presentations from telecommunications industry experts, followed by three panel sessions — “AI-Native 6G Networks,” “Taking 6G to Space With NTN,” and “Connecting the Physical and Digital Worlds” — along with technology demonstrations. Each session includes a lecture on the topic, as well as panel discussions that facilitate dynamic exchanges between participants.</p>



<p>The “AI-Native 6G Networks” session positions AI as the core technology driving innovation in network performance and operational efficiency, with discussion centered on the technical challenges and global collaboration needed to evolve 6G into a truly intelligent network infrastructure.</p>



<p>The “Taking 6G to Space With NTN” session reviews the current status and outlook of low Earth orbit (LEO) satellite constellations, discusses the technical barriers of space-based communication and satellite connectivity as a potential solution, and addresses satellite communication standards and roadmaps currently under discussion within the 3rd Generation Partnership Project (3GPP).</p>



<p>Concluding the day is the “Connecting the Physical and Digital Worlds” session, where participants are discussing new ways to deliver next-generation user experiences by sensing physical spaces and seamlessly linking them to digital services, such as Integrated Sensing and Communications (ISAC) and physical AI.</p>



<p>The technology demonstration session showcases cutting-edge 6G core technologies jointly developed and validated by Samsung and partner companies, including AI RAN implementations, eXtreme Multiple-Input Multiple-Output (X-MIMO) systems, Distributed MIMO (D-MIMO) technologies, and ISAC. Samsung is demonstrating field validation results for the performance of AI-enabled RAN across all layers, transmission performance in the 6G candidate frequency band (7 GHz band), the user experience at cell boundaries, and sensing performance that utilizes base station infrastructure.</p>



<figure class="wp-block-image size-full"><img loading="lazy" width="1000" height="563" src="https://img.global.news.samsung.com/global/wp-content/uploads/2026/10/15072848/Samsung-Corporate-Silicon-Valley-Future-Wireless-Summit-2026_main3.jpg" alt="An overview of the 6G core technology exhibition booth operated by Samsung Research at the event venue in Mountain View, Silicon Valley, on Oct. 14-15" class="wp-image-177766" /><figcaption class="wp-element-caption">▲ An overview of the 6G core technology exhibition booth operated by Samsung Research at the event venue in Mountain View, Silicon Valley, on Oct. 14-15</figcaption></figure>



<p>“Together, 6G and AI-native technologies represent the next step in telecommunications, bringing people closer together than ever before,” said Jinguk Jeong, Executive Vice President and Head of the Advanced Communications Research Center at Samsung Research. “The Silicon Valley Future Wireless Summit 2026 demonstrates our commitment to closely collaborating with industry partners to advance telecommunications.” </p>



<p></p>



<h2 class="wp-block-heading"><strong>Strengthening the Global 6G Ecosystem</strong></h2>



<p>Silicon Valley Future Wireless Summit 2026 and Partner Day come amid Samsung’s efforts to expand its collaboration on 6G and AI-native communication technology with global partners, including telecommunications operators, research institutes, and business groups.</p>



<p>This year, Samsung has initiated collaboration with LG U+ and conducted field validation with Verizon at a major international soccer fan event in Dallas, Texas, for ISAC technology development. The company is also strengthening research partnerships with major telecommunications operators globally, including NTT DOCOMO, KDDI Research, KT, SK Telecom, and SoftBank. These collaborations are part of ongoing efforts to make 6G technology a reality.</p>



<p>Samsung will continue to deepen collaboration with global partners and advance research at the convergence of AI and communications. By connecting its research strengths with business expertise, the company aims to translate innovation into greater value for the industry and reinforce its leadership in AI-native, 6G networks.</p> Samsung Expands Language Support Across AI Experiences on Galaxy https://news.samsung.com/global/samsung-expands-language-support-across-ai-experiences-on-galaxy Wed, 14 Oct 2026 21:00:00 +0000 Samsung Newsroom Mobile Bixby Galaxy AI https://bit.ly/3TUsiAT Samsung is expanding language support for Galaxy AI and Bixby, bringing these experiences to more users in their own language. Galaxy AI is expanding language support across more of its features, starting with those that translate, transcribe, and summarize. Call Assist, Note Assist, Interpreter, and Transcript Assist now include Hungarian and Malay, bringing features such […] <p>Samsung is expanding language support for Galaxy AI and Bixby, bringing these experiences to more users in their own language.</p>



<p>Galaxy AI is expanding language support across more of its features, starting with those that translate, transcribe, and summarize. Call Assist, Note Assist, Interpreter, and Transcript Assist now include Hungarian and Malay, bringing features such as Live Translate to 24 languages.<sup data-fn="2e40b666-acb9-43d8-9a86-24b5604b6c0f" class="fn"><a href="#2e40b666-acb9-43d8-9a86-24b5604b6c0f" id="2e40b666-acb9-43d8-9a86-24b5604b6c0f-link">1</a></sup></p>



<figure class="wp-block-image size-full"><img loading="lazy" width="1000" height="563" src="https://img.global.news.samsung.com/global/wp-content/uploads/2026/10/13150954/Samsung-Mobile-Language-Support-AI-Experience-Galaxy_Main1.jpg" alt="An image of a live translation of a phone call between Malay and English" class="wp-image-177726" /><figcaption class="wp-element-caption">▲ Live Translate of a phone call in Malay-English (Image simulated for illustrative purposes. Actual UX/UI may differ.)  </figcaption></figure>



<p>Elsewhere in Galaxy AI, Now Nudge, which suggests a relevant next step based on what is on screen, adds eight languages<sup data-fn="61261e38-c659-46b7-a074-d58a0b79f613" class="fn"><a href="#61261e38-c659-46b7-a074-d58a0b79f613" id="61261e38-c659-46b7-a074-d58a0b79f613-link">2</a></sup> to reach 24 as well.</p>



<p>Bixby is a device agent that offers intuitive device control through natural conversation. Users can ask for what they need in their own words, without having to know the exact name of a setting or where to find it in a menu. With the addition of Polish, Thai, and Vietnamese, Bixby now supports 12 languages,<sup data-fn="459dc8e3-bf42-4a97-8c7d-c843dc9a04f6" class="fn"><a href="#459dc8e3-bf42-4a97-8c7d-c843dc9a04f6" id="459dc8e3-bf42-4a97-8c7d-c843dc9a04f6-link">3</a></sup> allowing more Galaxy users to control their devices more effortlessly.</p>



<p>Bixby’s new languages will begin rolling out on October 14,<sup data-fn="9830fe21-b64f-415e-b442-0831d18c90e3" class="fn"><a href="#9830fe21-b64f-415e-b442-0831d18c90e3" id="9830fe21-b64f-415e-b442-0831d18c90e3-link">4</a></sup> with the new Galaxy AI languages to follow in the coming weeks.<sup data-fn="cabf23d1-a70b-4379-83f8-251e54938b2a" class="fn"><a href="#cabf23d1-a70b-4379-83f8-251e54938b2a" id="cabf23d1-a70b-4379-83f8-251e54938b2a-link">5</a></sup></p>



<hr class="wp-block-separator has-alpha-channel-opacity" />

The update also delivers more personalized sleep guidance and expanded running program tiers to help users build lasting, healthier routines. Sleep Coaching Becomes Easier To Start and More Personalized […] <p>Samsung Electronics today announced a major update to the Samsung Health app, making Sleep Coaching<sup data-fn="075efab3-b351-4413-9f90-de25a907367e" class="fn"><a href="#075efab3-b351-4413-9f90-de25a907367e" id="075efab3-b351-4413-9f90-de25a907367e-link">1</a></sup> even easier to start by lowering the data requirement to begin. The update also delivers more personalized sleep guidance and expanded running program tiers to help users build lasting, healthier routines.</p>



<p></p>



<h2 class="wp-block-heading"><strong>Sleep Coaching Becomes Easier To Start and More Personalized</strong></h2>



<figure class="wp-block-image size-full"><img loading="lazy" width="1000" height="563" src="https://img.global.news.samsung.com/global/wp-content/uploads/2026/10/13095013/Samsung-Mobile-Samsung-Health-Sleep-Coaching_Main1.jpg" alt="An image of Health App 7.1 Update for Sleep Coaching" class="wp-image-177699" /></figure>



<figure class="wp-block-video"><video playsinline autoplay controls loop muted src="https://img.global.news.samsung.com/global/wp-content/uploads/2026/10/13142206/Samsung-Mobile-Samsung-Health-Sleep-Coaching_Main1-2.mp4"></video></figure>



<p>While sleep tracking tells users how they slept, sleep coaching helps them sleep better by turning personal sleep goals and data into daily habits.</p>



<p>Samsung has made it even easier to begin sleep management. Users can now initiate Sleep Coaching with just three days of data within the past two weeks instead of seven days in a month.</p>



<p>During setup, users complete a survey on sleep regularity, sleep interruptions, alcohol and caffeine intake, and subjective sleep satisfaction. With these survey responses and three days of sleep data, the app assigns a personalized Sleep Animal symbol and builds a tailored four- to eight-week coaching program based on individual goals. As users progress, the program dynamically adapts its weekly recommendations to reflect changing sleep patterns, tracking daily sleep satisfaction and lifestyle shifts.</p>



<p>Informed by principles of behavioral sleep science, the program gives users daily habit checklists and sleep tips — making it easier to build healthier routines, such as avoiding screens an hour before bed, skipping late caffeine, and stretching outdoors after waking up.</p>



<p>Users can also enjoy more intuitive and detailed analysis reports. The new pentagon-shaped Sleep Balance graph consolidates five key aspects of sleep indicators — sleep time, sleep regularity, awake time during the night, sleep satisfaction, and Energy Score<sup data-fn="a52a608f-8127-4c5e-8c51-b615454ca53e" class="fn"><a href="#a52a608f-8127-4c5e-8c51-b615454ca53e" id="a52a608f-8127-4c5e-8c51-b615454ca53e-link">2</a></sup> — to provide an at-a-glance view of sleep trends and areas that may warrant attention, based on data collected or inputted through sleep coaching.</p>



<p>Morning daily insight messages, which review the previous night’s sleep with helpful tips, integrate closely with weekly and final reports to provide a comprehensive view of sleep trends. By linking daily actions with this data-driven progress, Samsung Health helps users establish sustainable lifestyle habits to support everyday wellness.</p>



<p></p>



<h2 class="wp-block-heading"><strong>Expanded Running Programs and Strava Routes Integration</strong></h2>



<figure class="wp-block-image size-full"><img loading="lazy" width="1000" height="563" src="https://img.global.news.samsung.com/global/wp-content/uploads/2026/10/13095032/Samsung-Mobile-Samsung-Health-Sleep-Coaching_Main2.jpg" alt="An image of Health App 7.1 Update for Running Coach" class="wp-image-177700" /></figure>



<p>In response to the growing popularity of running, Running Coach<sup data-fn="8c9edbba-6334-429e-a960-3ca8a06e1517" class="fn"><a href="#8c9edbba-6334-429e-a960-3ca8a06e1517" id="8c9edbba-6334-429e-a960-3ca8a06e1517-link">3</a></sup> has been upgraded to deliver a more dynamic and connected training experience.</p>



<p>Training tiers have expanded from 10 to 12 levels. While users continue to receive a personalized training plan based on a 12-minute running test or their historical running data, this update introduces new levels to support intermediate and advanced runners. These dedicated training tiers enable them to set higher benchmarks, challenge themselves, and steadily improve their performance.</p>



<p>To celebrate achievements and sustain motivation, Running Coach introduces an enhanced social media sharing feature. Runners can seamlessly export their workout data into share-ready templates designed for various social platforms, making it easier to share milestones, personal records, and route highlights with their fitness communities.</p>



<p>Samsung continues to strengthen ecosystem connectivity through a partnership with Strava,<sup data-fn="fdded9f2-bdfb-4406-b168-aaa569fd9434" class="fn"><a href="#fdded9f2-bdfb-4406-b168-aaa569fd9434" id="fdded9f2-bdfb-4406-b168-aaa569fd9434-link">4</a></sup> a global fitness platform. Users can import their favorite Strava Routes for running, trail running, and cycling directly into Samsung Health. This integration supports compatibility and user convenience across both platforms.</p>



<p>“Samsung Health’s personalized coaching empowers users to understand their health data, taking a step toward supporting everyday wellness,” said Hon Pak, Senior Vice President and Head of the Digital Health Team for the Mobile eXperience Business at Samsung Electronics. “We will continue to leverage the Galaxy ecosystem and our technology to build a trusted health platform.”</p>



<p>These upgraded features will be progressively rolled out via Samsung Health app updates starting in October.<sup data-fn="fc726e40-edd7-4038-84b9-6b4bc210953c" class="fn"><a href="#fc726e40-edd7-4038-84b9-6b4bc210953c" id="fc726e40-edd7-4038-84b9-6b4bc210953c-link">5</a></sup></p>

The collection includes Constable’s English countryside vistas, famous for the artist’s […] <figure class="wp-block-image size-full"><img loading="lazy" width="1000" height="558" src="https://img.global.news.samsung.com/global/wp-content/uploads/2026/10/01160619/Samsung-TVs-and-Displays-Samsung-Art-Store-British-Museum_main1.jpg" alt="" class="wp-image-177681" /><figcaption class="wp-element-caption">▲ “A Storm (Shipwreck)” by Joseph Mallord William Turner on The Frame Pro</figcaption></figure>



<p>Samsung Electronics today announced that it is adding a new exclusive British Museum collection consisting of 30 artworks to Samsung Art Store.<sup data-fn="0c050b58-46d4-4463-b594-702438de61c3" class="fn"><a href="#0c050b58-46d4-4463-b594-702438de61c3" id="0c050b58-46d4-4463-b594-702438de61c3-link">1</a></sup> Available on compatible Samsung Art TVs, the curated selection features works by John Constable, Joseph Mallord William Turner, and other renowned artists.</p>



<p>The collection includes Constable’s English countryside vistas, famous for the artist’s rare ability to portray shifting weather patterns in the sky. Turner’s watercolors, on the other hand, range from stormy seascapes to views of Venice, Rome, and Zurich.</p>



<p>“Samsung is committed to using our technology to make world-class art more accessible to everyone,” said Gwiho Lee, Executive Vice President of the Visual Display (VD) Business at Samsung Electronics. “By welcoming these iconic British Museum pieces into our platform, we are proud to offer our global audience an effortless way to appreciate historic art at home.”</p>



<figure class="wp-block-image size-full"><img loading="lazy" width="1000" height="671" src="https://img.global.news.samsung.com/global/wp-content/uploads/2026/10/01160811/Samsung-TVs-and-Displays-Samsung-Art-Store-British-Museum_main2.jpg" alt="" class="wp-image-177682" /><figcaption class="wp-element-caption">▲ “Fisherton Mill” by John Constable on The Frame Pro</figcaption></figure>



<p>Samsung Art Store subscribers will have access to the British Museum collection and will be able to simply browse the selection, choose a work to display on their TV, and switch to another work whenever they like.</p>



<p>“We’re delighted to work with Samsung to make more of the Museum’s collection available to people at home,” said Lisa San Martin, Interim Commercial Director of the British Museum. “We hope it encourages viewers to explore works they already know and discover others for the first time.”</p>



<p>The launch builds on Samsung’s relationship with the British Museum. Samsung is also a corporate partner of the museum’s “Korea” exhibition that opened on Oct. 1 and runs through Jan. 31, 2027.</p>



<p>The British Museum collection will be available on Samsung Art Store starting Oct. 6.</p>



<hr class="wp-block-separator has-alpha-channel-opacity" />



<h4 class="wp-block-heading"><strong>About The British Museum</strong><br>Founded in 1753, the British Museum was the first national public museum in the world. From the outset it was a museum of the world, for the world, and this idea still lies at the heart of the Museum’s mission today. The collection tells the stories of cultures across the world, from the dawn of human history, over two million years ago, to the present. Objects range from the earliest tools made by humans and treasures from the ancient world to more recent acquisitions from Africa, Oceania, and the Americas, the Middle East, Asia, and Europe, as well as the national collections of prints and drawings, and coins and medals.</h4>

The update introduces SmartThings Now at the top of the Home tab, refreshes the app’s Home tab experience, and expands local control capabilities — helping users […] <p>Samsung Electronics today announced the latest update to SmartThings, its global connected living platform now serving 470 million registered users and supporting 430+ Works with SmartThings (WWST)-certified brands worldwide. The update introduces SmartThings Now at the top of the Home tab, refreshes the app’s Home tab experience, and expands local control capabilities — helping users access relevant information, take action more easily, and manage connected devices with greater reliability.</p>



<p>“This update marks another step in SmartThings’ ongoing efforts to move beyond a home experience where users search for and launch features,” said Jaeyeon Jung, Executive Vice President and Head of the SmartThings Team, AI Platform Center, Samsung Electronics. “By delivering relevant information and controls at the right time, SmartThings is pushing the boundaries of more intelligent home experiences and empowering users to manage their everyday lives more naturally.”</p>



<figure class="wp-block-video"><video playsinline autoplay controls loop muted src="https://img.global.news.samsung.com/global/wp-content/uploads/2026/10/01154558/Samsung-Corporate-SmartThings-Now-New-Platform-Update_Main1.mp4"></video></figure>



<p></p>



<h2 class="wp-block-heading"><strong>A Redesigned Home Tab Experience, Led by SmartThings Now</strong></h2>



<p>SmartThings Now appears as cards at the top of the Home tab, building on Home Insight to offer greater personalization by combining relevant information with actions users can take right away. It suggests the right information and immediately actionable controls based on the user’s current situation, delivering an experience that is easier and more intuitive.</p>



<p>If a Family Care member, such as an older parent, shows no device activity for an extended period, SmartThings Now surfaces a care card for the caregiver with options to check in or call. When the user arrives near a home location pre-registered in the SmartThings app, SmartThings Now suggests turning on lights and air purifiers in advance. Once the user acts, it prompts them to create a routine so the same actions can run automatically going forward. This is all part of an experience designed to naturally guide users to the next relevant step based on their choices and context, making it easier to discover and use the features they need.</p>



<p>SmartThings Now also supports personalized optimization based on usage patterns. It analyzes the past week’s activity to suggest updates to Home tab Favorites, including devices, services, and routines. For example, it may recommend removing an air conditioner card as usage drops heading into cooler months, or adding an AirDresser card as the user’s air dresser becomes more frequently used during the change of seasons. Users can manage their routines through the Routine Insight card in SmartThings Now,<sup data-fn="00d495e4-e119-4a27-9522-9d4efc53c466" class="fn"><a href="#00d495e4-e119-4a27-9522-9d4efc53c466" id="00d495e4-e119-4a27-9522-9d4efc53c466-link">1</a></sup> as well, by navigating to a detailed view of their routine activity over the past week to review and delete routines they no longer need.</p>



<p>The updated Home tab reorganizes key information into a more streamlined view, covering dashboards, device cards, and service cards, with each type displayed in a format optimized for easier browsing.<sup data-fn="92120ae6-5a2b-48f8-b505-17ec6fa167fa" class="fn"><a href="#92120ae6-5a2b-48f8-b505-17ec6fa167fa" id="92120ae6-5a2b-48f8-b505-17ec6fa167fa-link">2</a></sup></p>



<p>Service cards use a more space-efficient design to clearly display more services on a single screen and present information like device status and numerical readings. Additionally, Live Wallpaper changes naturally throughout the day, adding a more dynamic visual touch to the experience.</p>



<p>The update also introduces a Floating Bar that places frequently used features within easier reach at the bottom of the screen, improving one-handed use on larger smartphones and reflecting the latest One UI design direction.</p>



<p></p>



<h2 class="wp-block-heading"><strong>Enhanced Local Control Helps Keep Devices Responsive, Even When Offline</strong></h2>



<p>SmartThings is now introducing local control technology that helps users continue to operate connected devices more reliably when there are changes to the home network environment or disruptions to external internet service.<sup data-fn="30a22357-dea7-49e0-8661-29185cb48b44" class="fn"><a href="#30a22357-dea7-49e0-8661-29185cb48b44" id="30a22357-dea7-49e0-8661-29185cb48b44-link">3</a></sup></p>



<p>When a mobile device and a SmartThings Hub are connected to the same Wi-Fi network, the mobile device can communicate directly with the hub rather than routing controls through the cloud. Compared to cloud-based control, local control can improve response times by approximately 30%. This helps enable continued control of connected devices during temporary network fluctuations.<sup data-fn="9893746f-d1d8-4998-bbe7-313c50aad3a5" class="fn"><a href="#9893746f-d1d8-4998-bbe7-313c50aad3a5" id="9893746f-d1d8-4998-bbe7-313c50aad3a5-link">4</a></sup></p>



<p>SmartThings automatically switches between local and cloud-based remote control based on whether the user’s mobile device is at home or away. The updated Offline Device Diagnostics page helps users restore devices that have gone offline by updating new network information across all registered devices at once.</p>



<p>Samsung has also added support for Matter 1.6, the latest smart home connectivity standard from the Connectivity Standards Alliance (CSA), enhancing stability and Group Control performance. The update adds support for four new device categories, as well:</p>



<ul class="wp-block-list">
<li>Energy Meters for real-time power monitoring</li>



<li>Closures for specialized door and gate types</li>



<li>Irrigation devices such as sprinkler systems</li>



<li>Soil Sensors for measuring ground conditions.</li>
</ul>



<hr class="wp-block-separator has-alpha-channel-opacity" />


<ol class="wp-block-footnotes"><li id="00d495e4-e119-4a27-9522-9d4efc53c466">Routine Insight is accessible through SmartThings Now and the Automations > Suggestions tab in the SmartThings app. <a href="#00d495e4-e119-4a27-9522-9d4efc53c466-link" aria-label="Jump to footnote reference 1"><img src="https://s.w.org/images/core/emoji/16.0.1/72x72/21a9.png" alt="↩" class="wp-smiley" style="height: 1em; max-height: 1em;" />︎</a></li><li id="92120ae6-5a2b-48f8-b505-17ec6fa167fa">The updated Home tab experience, including SmartThings Now, is available across SmartThings-supported devices such as mobile devices, Samsung Smart TVs, Family Hub refrigerators, Samsung appliances with 7-inch or 9-inch screens, and Windows devices. <a href="#92120ae6-5a2b-48f8-b505-17ec6fa167fa-link" aria-label="Jump to footnote reference 2"><img src="https://s.w.org/images/core/emoji/16.0.1/72x72/21a9.png" alt="↩" class="wp-smiley" style="height: 1em; max-height: 1em;" />︎</a></li><li id="30a22357-dea7-49e0-8661-29185cb48b44">Local control is available on Android and iOS devices with the SmartThings app installed, for SmartThings Station, SmartThings V2, V3, and V4 Hubs, and hub-enabled Samsung Smart TVs and Family Hub. <a href="#30a22357-dea7-49e0-8661-29185cb48b44-link" aria-label="Jump to footnote reference 3"><img src="https://s.w.org/images/core/emoji/16.0.1/72x72/21a9.png" alt="↩" class="wp-smiley" style="height: 1em; max-height: 1em;" />︎</a></li><li id="9893746f-d1d8-4998-bbe7-313c50aad3a5">Based on tests conducted by Samsung under controlled conditions. Actual performance may vary depending on the user’s environment. <a href="#9893746f-d1d8-4998-bbe7-313c50aad3a5-link" aria-label="Jump to footnote reference 4"><img src="https://s.w.org/images/core/emoji/16.0.1/72x72/21a9.png" alt="↩" class="wp-smiley" style="height: 1em; max-height: 1em;" />︎</a></li></ol> Lee Kun-Hee Collection Expands to Europe With Opening of ‘Korea’ Exhibition at the British Museum https://news.samsung.com/global/lee-kun-hee-collection-expands-to-europe-with-opening-of-korea-exhibition-at-the-british-museum Thu, 01 Oct 2026 10:00:00 +0000 Samsung Newsroom More Stories Press Releases British Museum Lee Kun-Hee https://bit.ly/4xYKrvs Samsung Electronics, in partnership with the British Museum, hosted a gala event on Sept. 30 at the museum to commemorate the opening of “Korea” exhibition, a major showcasing of works from the late Samsung Chairman Lee Kun-Hee’s renowned art collection. Following successful exhibitions in Washington D.C. and Chicago, it marks the European stop of the […] <p>Samsung Electronics, in partnership with the British Museum, hosted a gala event on Sept. 30 at the museum to commemorate the opening of “Korea” exhibition, a major showcasing of works from the late Samsung Chairman Lee Kun-Hee’s renowned art collection.</p>



<p>Following successful exhibitions in Washington D.C. and Chicago, it marks the European stop of the collection’s international tour. It is also the first major special presentation of Korean art at the British Museum in more than 40 years. The exhibition runs from Oct. 1, 2026 to Jan. 31, 2027, and is being held at the Joseph Hotung Great Court Gallery.</p>



<p>“Korea,” spanning over two millennia, connect the history of Korean art in a unified narrative to reveal how Korea’s unique aesthetic values and artistic achievements have continued to be built upon and reinterpreted over many generations.</p>



<p>The exhibition, drawn from over 23,000 artworks from the National Bequest of the Lee Kun-Hee Collection in 2021, is organised by the British Museum, the National Museum of Korea, and the National Museum of Modern and Contemporary Art, Korea.</p>



<p>At the gala, Samsung Electronics Executive Chairman Jay Y. Lee reflected on the Lee family’s multigenerational commitment to preserving Korea’s cultural heritage and sharing it with audiences around the world. Members of the family, including Hong Ra-Hee, Director Emerita of the Leeum Museum of Art, Lee Boo-jin, President & CEO of Hotel Shilla, and Lee Seo-hyun, President of Samsung C&T, also attended the event.</p>



<p>“For generations, my family has held a guiding principle that art and culture should be preserved and shared,” Executive Chairman Lee said. “I hope this exhibition offers a gateway into Korean culture, spanning two thousand years of creativity and heritage, and that it will let everyone see Korea a little differently — for its beauty, its depth and its resilience.”</p>



<p>At the reception held before the gala dinner, Her Royal Highness The Princess Royal graced the opening of the exhibition.</p>



<p>Among the 240 guests were the Chancellor of the Exchequer John Healey MP, Minister for Artificial Intelligence Kanishka Narayan MP, former Deputy Prime Minister Sir Oliver Dowden, former Trade Minister Sir Greg Hands, former Culture Secretary Sir John Whittingdale, Trade Envoy to the Republic of Korea John Slinger, Leader of the Liberal Democrats and Chair of the Republic of Korea All-Party Parliamentary Group Sir Ed Davey, and Dr. Ben Spencer, Vice Chair of the Republic of Korea All-Party Parliamentary Group.</p>



<p>George Osborne, Chair of the British Museum, said, “We are thrilled here at the British Museum to be hosting this incredible exhibition. The Lee family’s contribution to our shared cultural heritage helps us understand and interpret the past, so that we may understand the future.”</p>



<p>Maurice Lévy, Chairman Emeritus of Publicis Groupe, said that the exhibition is “not simply about objects from witnesses of a great past of Korea. They are evidence of a culture that has always valued craftsmanship, imagination, discipline, and excellence. Sharing those treasures with the world speaks volume about the values and the generosity of the Lee family. They understood, generation after generation, that looking forward does not mean forgetting where you come from.”</p>



<p>Kanishka Narayan, Minister for Artificial Intelligence, said, “The UK and Korea work closely on technology and innovation, but there is so much more to our relationship. Korea’s art and culture are admired around the world, and this exhibition gives people in Britain a chance to explore its extraordinary artistic heritage. I’m grateful to Samsung for bringing this remarkable collection to London and sharing it with audiences here.”</p>



<p>Kim Heung-chong, Ambassador of the Republic of Korea to the U.K., commented that “it is particularly meaningful for the British Museum to open this exhibition of artworks showcasing two thousand years of Korean history, at a time when K-culture is enjoying global recognition,” and thanked Executive Chairman Lee for making the exhibition possible.</p>



<p>Business leaders attending the gala included those from BT Group, Vodafone, Sainsbury’s, and Standard Chartered. From academia, representatives from the University of Oxford and Imperial College London were in attendance, while the cultural sector was represented by the British Museum, Tate Modern, the V&A, and Lisson Gallery, alongside contemporary artist Marc Quinn.</p>



<p>Samsung’s business partners from the semiconductor industry, including Christophe Fouquet, CEO of ASML, Kai Beckmann, CEO of Merck, Andreas Pecher, CEO of ZEISS, and Patrick Vandenameele, CEO of imec, were among other notable attendees.</p>



<p></p>



<h2 class="wp-block-heading"><strong>Chairman Lee Expresses Gratitude to Korean War Veterans</strong></h2>



<p>During his remarks, Executive Chairman Lee took a special moment to honor the three Korean War veterans in attendance, including Mike Roe. He also thanked The Princess Royal, recognising her visit to Korea in July 2026 during which she honored the veterans. Reflecting on more than 140 years of ties between Korea and the U.K., he highlighted Samsung’s 80-year business relationship with the country and emphasised that the U.K. has served as the hub for its European operations for over five decades.</p>



<p>The London exhibition features 100 masterpieces, including 10 National Treasures and 13 Treasures of Korea, with more than twenty pieces being revealed for the first time internationally.</p>



<p>Among the newly presented works are the National Treasures “Bronze Artifacts from Bisan-dong, Daegu” and “Bronze Rattles,” as well as “Celadon Incense Burner with Girin-shaped Lid.” Among the modern and contemporary works, Nam June Paik’s “Brahms,” Lee Jungseop’s “Bound People,” and Park Rehyun’s “Woman” will also be shown for the first time.</p>



<p>The Lee family’s commitment to preserving and sharing Korea’s culture spans three generations. It began with Samsung founder Lee Byung-Chull, who built the foundation of the collection and believed that cultural heritage should be passed on to future generations.</p>



<p>His son, the late Samsung Chairman Lee Kun-Hee carried this legacy forward, building and preserving the collection over decades. He viewed cultural heritage not simply as something to be owned, but as something to be protected and preserved. Director Emerita Hong Ra-Hee also played a crucial role in expanding and developing the collection to encompass both ancient and modern works.</p>



<p>The Lee Kun-Hee Collection, now a national cultural asset, is reaching audiences in major cities around the world from Washington D.C. to Chicago to London, enabling them to experience and appreciate the value and historical significance of Korean culture, thereby elevating Korea’s global standing.</p>



<p>The collection’s global tour began in November 2025 at the Smithsonian’s National Museum of Asian Art in Washington D.C., attracting about 80,000 visitors to become the museum’s largest special exhibition in the past 10 years. The collection then traveled to the Art Institute of Chicago, where it drew approximately 115,000 visitors, highlighting the strong interest in the rich history of Korean arts and culture.</p>



<p>Samsung remains committed to introducing Korean cultural heritage to the world while continuing to expand its global business network to support future growth and innovation.</p>

<figure class="wp-block-image size-full"><img loading="lazy" width="1000" height="750" src="https://img.global.news.samsung.com/global/wp-content/uploads/2026/10/01084559/Samsung-Corporate-Lee-Kun-Hee-Collection-British-Museum-Korea-Exhibition_main8.jpg" alt="Executive Chairman Lee at the gala" class="wp-image-177656" /></figure> Samsung Galaxy SmartTag3 Makes It Easier To Keep Track of What Matters https://news.samsung.com/global/samsung-galaxy-smarttag3-makes-it-easier-to-keep-track-of-what-matters Wed, 30 Sep 2026 21:00:00 +0000 Samsung Newsroom Mobile Press Releases Galaxy Find Network Galaxy SmartTag3 SmartThings Find https://bit.ly/4dvbqr4 Samsung Electronics today announced the new Galaxy SmartTag3, a compact tracker that combines a smaller, lighter design with longer-range finding, cross-OS support, faster location updates through the Galaxy Find Network and expanded proactive alerts. “People want simpler and more convenient ways to keep track of belongings and pets for greater peace of mind, and Galaxy SmartTag3 […] <figure class="wp-block-image size-full"><img loading="lazy" width="1000" height="660" src="https://img.global.news.samsung.com/global/wp-content/uploads/2026/09/23153223/Samsung-Mobile-Galaxy-SmartTag3-Galaxy-Find-Network-Place-Notification_main1.jpg" alt="Galaxy SmartTag3" class="wp-image-177431" /></figure>



<p>Samsung Electronics today announced the new Galaxy SmartTag3, a compact tracker that combines a smaller, lighter design with longer-range finding, cross-OS support, faster location updates through the Galaxy Find Network and expanded proactive alerts.<sup data-fn="6d7fe0ff-3f00-46bc-b7b5-c6c2231edd59" class="fn"><a href="#6d7fe0ff-3f00-46bc-b7b5-c6c2231edd59" id="6d7fe0ff-3f00-46bc-b7b5-c6c2231edd59-link">1</a></sup></p>



<p>“People want simpler and more convenient ways to keep track of belongings and pets for greater peace of mind, and Galaxy SmartTag3 was designed around those everyday needs,” said Jay Kim, Executive Vice President and Head of Customer Experience Office, Mobile eXperience (MX) Business at Samsung Electronics. “Powered by the vast Galaxy Find Network, it delivers our most capable Galaxy SmartTag experience yet.”</p>



<p></p>



<h2 class="wp-block-heading"><strong>Versatile Design, More Possibilities</strong></h2>



<p>Galaxy SmartTag3 introduces a compact design that is approximately 35% smaller by volume and 33% lighter than the previous generation,<sup data-fn="28c667cb-9b0c-4247-9853-26029e9a2a0d" class="fn"><a href="#28c667cb-9b0c-4247-9853-26029e9a2a0d" id="28c667cb-9b0c-4247-9853-26029e9a2a0d-link">2</a></sup> making it easier to attach to keys, smaller bags, luggage, or a pet collar.<sup data-fn="8b63462e-1c6c-435f-9747-ab2b7e6d850f" class="fn"><a href="#8b63462e-1c6c-435f-9747-ab2b7e6d850f" id="8b63462e-1c6c-435f-9747-ab2b7e6d850f-link">3</a></sup> Its rounded form is finished with an aluminum-decorated button for a refined, durable feel, while new official accessories including a Silicone Case with a keyring and a Loop Case offer even more flexibility.<sup data-fn="6bd32861-9144-4e68-946e-c5fb73f8272f" class="fn"><a href="#6bd32861-9144-4e68-946e-c5fb73f8272f" id="6bd32861-9144-4e68-946e-c5fb73f8272f-link">4</a></sup></p>



<p>For the first time in the Galaxy SmartTag lineup, Galaxy SmartTag3 also brings <strong>cross-OS compatibility </strong>for Galaxy and iOS devices, making it easier for families and groups to access core finding and sharing features across platforms. Through SmartThings Find, users can check and share a tag’s location and use Search Nearby across supported devices.<sup data-fn="eb1d0e5b-173c-499f-a6af-4ff9972f4e69" class="fn"><a href="#eb1d0e5b-173c-499f-a6af-4ff9972f4e69" id="eb1d0e5b-173c-499f-a6af-4ff9972f4e69-link">5</a></sup> This is especially useful when a friend or family member borrows a suitcase for a trip, allowing them to check its location and use supported finding features from their own Galaxy or iOS device.</p>



<p>Galaxy SmartTag3 offers industry-leading battery life, with up to 550 days under standard usage and up to 790 days in Power Saving Mode.<sup data-fn="9c4874d0-2b42-4fb1-b7fc-265a45f5909f" class="fn"><a href="#9c4874d0-2b42-4fb1-b7fc-265a45f5909f" id="9c4874d0-2b42-4fb1-b7fc-265a45f5909f-link">6</a></sup></p>



<p></p>



<h2 class="wp-block-heading"><strong>See Further, Find Faster</strong></h2>



<p>When something goes missing, knowing where to start looking can make all the difference. Galaxy SmartTag3 significantly expands the range of<strong> Compass View,</strong><sup data-fn="facec774-1c4f-40d8-9e79-41eb50beff75" class="fn"><a href="#facec774-1c4f-40d8-9e79-41eb50beff75" id="facec774-1c4f-40d8-9e79-41eb50beff75-link">7</a></sup> helping users begin narrowing in on an item from farther away.</p>



<p>Bluetooth 6.0 Channel Sounding enables Compass View to provide distance and directional guidance from up to 60 meters away — around three times farther than the previous generation. As users get closer, ultra-wideband (UWB) delivers even greater precision, creating a more continuous finding experience from long range to close range. If a set of keys goes missing somewhere in a large park, Compass View can help users narrow in on its location from farther away and guide them more precisely as they get closer.</p>



<p>When Galaxy SmartTag3 moves beyond direct connection range, the <strong>Galaxy</strong> <strong>Find Network</strong> helps keep the search going. More than <strong>900 million</strong> participating Galaxy devices worldwide can act as finding nodes, helping detect Galaxy SmartTag3 and update its location beyond the reach of the user’s own phone.<sup data-fn="32863306-4a46-4541-9c92-88d882c4d29c" class="fn"><a href="#32863306-4a46-4541-9c92-88d882c4d29c" id="32863306-4a46-4541-9c92-88d882c4d29c-link">8</a></sup> Faster Galaxy Find Network updates can surface new location information sooner after disconnection, helping reduce uncertainty when luggage or another tagged belonging is unexpectedly separated from its owner.</p>



<p></p>



<h2 class="wp-block-heading"><strong>Stay Informed Proactively</strong></h2>



<p>Galaxy SmartTag3 goes beyond finding by helping users stay informed without actively searching. The new <strong>Place Notification</strong><sup data-fn="933f5484-c78b-46f6-b283-ad5a924ac190" class="fn"><a href="#933f5484-c78b-46f6-b283-ad5a924ac190" id="933f5484-c78b-46f6-b283-ad5a924ac190-link">9</a></sup> feature adds a proactive layer, letting users define an area and receive an alert when a tagged item moves in or out of it.</p>



<p>Using location data from the Galaxy Find Network, <strong>Place Notification</strong> can notify users when a family pet leaves the garden or when a bicycle is detected moving out of its usual parking area. Instead of requiring users to repeatedly check a location, Galaxy SmartTag3 can surface the information when it becomes relevant.</p>



<p><strong>Location History</strong><sup data-fn="fd1b5530-9105-43d4-ae0c-b650cae85e96" class="fn"><a href="#fd1b5530-9105-43d4-ae0c-b650cae85e96" id="fd1b5530-9105-43d4-ae0c-b650cae85e96-link">10</a></sup> lets users review where their tag has been, while <strong>Movement Detection Alert</strong><sup data-fn="a26f7c55-53db-47bf-a93e-0031620969ba" class="fn"><a href="#a26f7c55-53db-47bf-a93e-0031620969ba" id="a26f7c55-53db-47bf-a93e-0031620969ba-link">11</a></sup><strong> </strong>can notify them when movement is detected so they can stay informed without actively checking.<sup data-fn="bf00bae1-c003-4030-aec2-57e8846f37fe" class="fn"><a href="#bf00bae1-c003-4030-aec2-57e8846f37fe" id="bf00bae1-c003-4030-aec2-57e8846f37fe-link">12</a></sup></p>



<p></p>



<h2 class="wp-block-heading"><strong>Built for Everyday Privacy and Confidence</strong></h2>



<p>Galaxy SmartTag3 retains an IP67<sup data-fn="580d43fe-56bd-4454-817b-91eb9edab15e" class="fn"><a href="#580d43fe-56bd-4454-817b-91eb9edab15e" id="580d43fe-56bd-4454-817b-91eb9edab15e-link">13</a></sup> water and dust resistance rating alongside other key Galaxy SmartTag capabilities:</p>



<ul class="wp-block-list">
<li>NFC-powered <strong>Lost Mode,</strong><sup data-fn="30aea358-de52-47fb-94b4-7e44f3208e10" class="fn"><a href="#30aea358-de52-47fb-94b4-7e44f3208e10" id="30aea358-de52-47fb-94b4-7e44f3208e10-link">14</a></sup> which can display registered contact information or a short message when a lost tag is scanned with a compatible mobile device.</li>



<li><strong>SmartThings IoT Control</strong>, which allows users to trigger preset routines for compatible connected devices using the tag button.</li>



<li><strong>Left Behind Alert</strong>,<sup data-fn="5086c1cc-6b02-48ed-9341-6ce908e6010a" class="fn"><a href="#5086c1cc-6b02-48ed-9341-6ce908e6010a" id="5086c1cc-6b02-48ed-9341-6ce908e6010a-link">15</a></sup> which can notify users when their mobile device loses connection with a tagged belonging.</li>
</ul>



<p>Privacy and security remain central to the Galaxy SmartTag experience with location data that is encrypted to help protect user privacy.</p>



<p></p>



<h2 class="wp-block-heading"><strong>Availability</strong></h2>



<p>Samsung Galaxy SmartTag3 will be available first in Korea beginning Oct. 7, 2026, followed by the U.S., with additional markets to follow in 2027. It will be offered in White and Black.</p>



<p>For more information, please visit <strong>samsung.com</strong> or the Samsung Newsroom.</p>

For Bluetooth Channel Sounding, an up-to-60 m detection range is measured under line-of-sight (LOS) conditions, with no obstacles between the transmitter and receiver. Its detection range and the accuracy of direction and distance measurements may be reduced by nearby electronic devices, walls and other physical obstructions. Availability may vary by country, region, and device. <a href="#facec774-1c4f-40d8-9e79-41eb50beff75-link" aria-label="Jump to footnote reference 7"><img src="https://s.w.org/images/core/emoji/16.0.1/72x72/21a9.png" alt="↩" class="wp-smiley" style="height: 1em; max-height: 1em;" />︎</a></li><li id="32863306-4a46-4541-9c92-88d882c4d29c">Galaxy Find Network availability and location update timing depend on nearby participating Galaxy devices, network conditions, device settings and regional conditions. <a href="#32863306-4a46-4541-9c92-88d882c4d29c-link" aria-label="Jump to footnote reference 8"><img src="https://s.w.org/images/core/emoji/16.0.1/72x72/21a9.png" alt="↩" class="wp-smiley" style="height: 1em; max-height: 1em;" />︎</a></li><li id="933f5484-c78b-46f6-b283-ad5a924ac190">Place Notification feature is a trial feature. To enable the feature, go to SmartThings > Life > Find, then turn on Notify when in set place. The displayed location of Galaxy SmartTag3 may differ from its actual location depending on its surroundings. <a href="#933f5484-c78b-46f6-b283-ad5a924ac190-link" aria-label="Jump to footnote reference 9"><img src="https://s.w.org/images/core/emoji/16.0.1/72x72/21a9.png" alt="↩" class="wp-smiley" style="height: 1em; max-height: 1em;" />︎</a></li><li id="fd1b5530-9105-43d4-ae0c-b650cae85e96">Location history is stored for up to 7 days. Older records will automatically be deleted. <a href="#fd1b5530-9105-43d4-ae0c-b650cae85e96-link" aria-label="Jump to footnote reference 10"><img src="https://s.w.org/images/core/emoji/16.0.1/72x72/21a9.png" alt="↩" class="wp-smiley" style="height: 1em; max-height: 1em;" />︎</a></li><li id="a26f7c55-53db-47bf-a93e-0031620969ba">Notify When Movement Detected feature operates only when Galaxy SmartTag3 is disconnected from the device and relies on signals detected by nearby Galaxy devices that have agreed to Offline finding. Notifications may not be triggered depending on the surroundings of Galaxy SmartTag3. <a href="#a26f7c55-53db-47bf-a93e-0031620969ba-link" aria-label="Jump to footnote reference 11"><img src="https://s.w.org/images/core/emoji/16.0.1/72x72/21a9.png" alt="↩" class="wp-smiley" style="height: 1em; max-height: 1em;" />︎</a></li><li id="bf00bae1-c003-4030-aec2-57e8846f37fe">Depending on the availability and status of nearby Galaxy devices that have agreed to Offline finding, there may be a delay in sending or receiving notifications, or notifications may not be received at all. Notifications may not be triggered depending on the surroundings of Galaxy SmartTag3. <a href="#bf00bae1-c003-4030-aec2-57e8846f37fe-link" aria-label="Jump to footnote reference 12"><img src="https://s.w.org/images/core/emoji/16.0.1/72x72/21a9.png" alt="↩" class="wp-smiley" style="height: 1em; max-height: 1em;" />︎</a></li><li id="580d43fe-56bd-4454-817b-91eb9edab15e">Consistent with IP67 rating, water resistant in up to 1 m (3.3 feet) of water for up to 30 minutes. Rinse residue / dry after wet. Not advised for beach or pool use. Water and dust resistance is not permanent and may diminish over time. <a href="#580d43fe-56bd-4454-817b-91eb9edab15e-link" aria-label="Jump to footnote reference 13"><img src="https://s.w.org/images/core/emoji/16.0.1/72x72/21a9.png" alt="↩" class="wp-smiley" style="height: 1em; max-height: 1em;" />︎</a></li><li id="30aea358-de52-47fb-94b4-7e44f3208e10">Contact information and message must be registered beforehand on the SmartThings app to have it shown on the finder’s device on Lost Mode. The finder’s device must support NFC reading and be connected to the internet to display the pre-registered contact information upon tagging. To help protect your personal information, it is recommended that Lost Mode be turned off when it is no longer needed. <a href="#30aea358-de52-47fb-94b4-7e44f3208e10-link" aria-label="Jump to footnote reference 14"><img src="https://s.w.org/images/core/emoji/16.0.1/72x72/21a9.png" alt="↩" class="wp-smiley" style="height: 1em; max-height: 1em;" />︎</a></li><li id="5086c1cc-6b02-48ed-9341-6ce908e6010a">Notify When Left Behind feature relies entirely on the connection to your device. False alerts may occur if obstacles or environmental factors disrupt the connection, even when the item is still nearby. Feature availability may vary by platform, device and market. Left Behind Alert with SmartTag3 is currently not available on iOS. <a href="#5086c1cc-6b02-48ed-9341-6ce908e6010a-link" aria-label="Jump to footnote reference 15"><img src="https://s.w.org/images/core/emoji/16.0.1/72x72/21a9.png" alt="↩" class="wp-smiley" style="height: 1em; max-height: 1em;" />︎</a></li></ol> Samsung Introduces Galaxy Tab S12 Series: The Ultimate Productivity Powerhouse Built for Growth https://news.samsung.com/global/samsung-introduces-galaxy-tab-s12-series-the-ultimate-productivity-powerhouse-built-for-growth Wed, 30 Sep 2026 21:00:00 +0000 Samsung Newsroom Mobile Press Releases Galaxy AI Galaxy Tab S12 series Galaxy Tab S12 Ultra Galaxy Tab S12+ One UI 9 https://bit.ly/46AjFhQ Samsung Electronics today unveiled the Galaxy Tab S12 series — Galaxy Tab S12 Ultra and Tab S12+ — delivering Samsung’s most capable and versatile tablet experience yet. Packing breakthrough performance, long-lasting battery life, and the latest Galaxy AI into Galaxy Tab’s signature ultra-thin design, the new lineup turns complex workflows into effortless, everyday achievements whether […] <p>Samsung Electronics today unveiled the <strong>Galaxy Tab S12 series</strong> — Galaxy Tab S12 Ultra and Tab S12+ — delivering Samsung’s most capable and versatile tablet experience yet. Packing breakthrough performance, long-lasting battery life, and the latest Galaxy AI<sup data-fn="a285aea1-ee4c-427c-b818-31756efdbbcc" class="fn"><a href="#a285aea1-ee4c-427c-b818-31756efdbbcc" id="a285aea1-ee4c-427c-b818-31756efdbbcc-link">1</a></sup> into Galaxy Tab’s signature ultra-thin design, the new lineup turns complex workflows into effortless, everyday achievements whether it’s mastering skills, pursuing career goals, or exploring creative passions.</p>



<figure class="wp-block-image size-full"><img loading="lazy" width="1000" height="662" src="https://img.global.news.samsung.com/global/wp-content/uploads/2026/09/28100811/Samsung-Mobile-Galaxy-Tab-S12-Series_Main1.jpg" alt="An Image of Galaxy Tab S12 series Combo " class="wp-image-177451" /></figure>



<p>Galaxy Tab S12 Ultra and Tab S12+ feature immersive displays and come bundled with the ergonomically designed S Pen for effortless note-taking and precise editing. With easy access to professional-grade apps and versatile accessories, users can pursue their passions and progress toward their goals with ease. Combining portability with professional-grade power, the Galaxy Tab S12 series gives users instant access to powerful capabilities whenever inspiration strikes.</p>



<p>“The Galaxy Tab S12 series is designed for people who are constantly exploring new ideas, developing new skills, and bringing their ambitions to life,” said Jay Kim, Executive Vice President and Head of Customer Experience Office, Mobile eXperience (MX) Business at Samsung Electronics. “Built with industry-leading hardware, the Galaxy Tab S12 series pairs an expansive screen with the power of Galaxy AI — removing friction from complex tasks so users can stay focused on achieving impactful results.”</p>



<p></p>



<h2 class="wp-block-heading"><strong>Peak Performance in an Ultra-Thin Design</strong></h2>



<p>The Galaxy Tab S12 series combines powerful performance and long-lasting battery life into a thin and light design, making it easy for users to achieve more, wherever ambition takes them. At a remarkably slim 5.1mm, Galaxy Tab S12 Ultra is Samsung’s thinnest Galaxy Tab, while delivering the most powerful performance of any Galaxy Tab to date, supporting productive use throughout the day. For example, users taking an online course alongside a full-time job can catch up on a lesson during the commute, then work through course exercises on the large screen at a café or library later in the day.</p>

<p>Both models are built to go the distance, allowing users to keep going without losing momentum. On a single charge, the all-day battery lasts up to 23 hours on Galaxy Tab S12 Ultra<sup data-fn="4efa14cf-5583-4213-91d1-7ac0fe6221a1" class="fn"><a href="#4efa14cf-5583-4213-91d1-7ac0fe6221a1" id="4efa14cf-5583-4213-91d1-7ac0fe6221a1-link">2</a></sup> and up to 21 hours on Galaxy Tab S12+,<sup data-fn="473de610-f0d0-46fb-9012-a0784ac7ec6f" class="fn"><a href="#473de610-f0d0-46fb-9012-a0784ac7ec6f" id="473de610-f0d0-46fb-9012-a0784ac7ec6f-link">3</a></sup> supporting long stretches of productivity anywhere. When it is time to recharge, the battery goes from 0% to 100% in about 95 minutes with the 45W adapter — minimizing downtime so users can get back to what they were doing.<sup data-fn="79643b47-aeef-46cc-b53f-1c80dcc11b07" class="fn"><a href="#79643b47-aeef-46cc-b53f-1c80dcc11b07" id="79643b47-aeef-46cc-b53f-1c80dcc11b07-link">4</a></sup></p>



<p>Beyond long-lasting battery life, the Galaxy Tab S12 series delivers the most powerful performance of any Galaxy Tab to date, keeping pace with even intensive tasks. The cutting-edge 3nm processor delivers up to 61% faster NPU processing, 19% faster CPU performance, and 17% improved GPU power compared to Galaxy Tab S11 Ultra.<sup data-fn="d4e5b51e-0431-4415-89ca-d75e99947b93" class="fn"><a href="#d4e5b51e-0431-4415-89ca-d75e99947b93" id="d4e5b51e-0431-4415-89ca-d75e99947b93-link">5</a></sup> In everyday use, that means users can take on more demanding tasks with increased speed and responsiveness. Whether rendering complex creative projects or watching a lecture while taking notes and asking AI for clarification — the experience stays responsive and fast with fewer interruptions to break their focus.</p>



<p>A newly designed <strong>3D Vapor Chamber</strong> efficiently dissipates heat to support more reliable performance during demanding tasks, whether users are editing video or designing graphics for hours.<sup data-fn="12cce296-f782-4448-83ab-b3999853b00d" class="fn"><a href="#12cce296-f782-4448-83ab-b3999853b00d" id="12cce296-f782-4448-83ab-b3999853b00d-link">6</a></sup> Engineered for resilience and durability to withstand the elements and give users peace of mind, the Galaxy Tab S12 series also comes with <strong>IP68-rated water and dust resistance</strong>.<sup data-fn="1fca1dce-5905-4002-8a43-9a887370074b" class="fn"><a href="#1fca1dce-5905-4002-8a43-9a887370074b" id="1fca1dce-5905-4002-8a43-9a887370074b-link">7</a></sup></p>



<p>The Galaxy Tab S12 series comes with an expansive screen designed to help users think bigger and give ideas more room to unfold. Galaxy Tab S12 Ultra features a large 14.6-inch display, making it easy to explore complex topics and keep multiple perspectives in view. The Galaxy Tab S12+ features a larger 12.6-inch display and slimmer bezels than its predecessor, offering more screen space without sacrificing portability. Both models feature <strong>Dynamic AMOLED 2X</strong> displays reaching up to 1,600 nits of peak brightness<sup data-fn="7c8890d6-fa9d-4844-bb9f-ea2d0ae94cb0" class="fn"><a href="#7c8890d6-fa9d-4844-bb9f-ea2d0ae94cb0" id="7c8890d6-fa9d-4844-bb9f-ea2d0ae94cb0-link">8</a></sup> — delivering clear, vivid visuals indoors, or out. The upgraded seamless <strong>Vision Booster</strong> now adjusts brightness in real time based on lighting conditions for comfortable viewing.</p>



<figure class="wp-block-image size-full"><img loading="lazy" width="1000" height="562" src="https://img.global.news.samsung.com/global/wp-content/uploads/2026/09/28102516/Samsung-Mobile-Galaxy-Tab-S12-Series_Main3.jpg" alt="An image of Display " class="wp-image-177455" /></figure>



<p>Sound gets a major upgrade as well, with <strong>4-channel spatial audio</strong> — twice as many channels as the previous generation — delivering a richer, more immersive experience whether users are streaming content, following a tutorial, or editing.</p>



<p></p>



<h2 class="wp-block-heading"><strong>Making Everyday Tasks More Intuitive and Effortless</strong></h2>



<p>Building on an industry-leading hardware foundation, the Galaxy Tab S12 series turns its large screen into a flexible workspace that makes it easy to keep up with different tasks without missing a beat. Running Android 17 with <strong>One UI 9 </strong>and the<strong> latest Galaxy AI</strong>, it brings research, note-taking, creation, and AI assistance into one place, giving users more freedom to explore new ideas, capture what matters and put what they learn into practice.</p>



<p>Thanks to the Galaxy Tab S12 series’ immersive screen, users can split the screen into up to three sections in <strong>Multi Window</strong> layout. For example, users can take notes in <strong>Samsung Notes</strong> on one side while playing a lecture and opening an AI assistant on the other. As key takeaways or questions come up, users can capture them with the <strong>S Pen</strong> directly in Samsung Notes, while <strong>Note Assist</strong><sup data-fn="13c82efd-0ab3-4dbf-859e-0551f174352c" class="fn"><a href="#13c82efd-0ab3-4dbf-859e-0551f174352c" id="13c82efd-0ab3-4dbf-859e-0551f174352c-link">9</a></sup> summarizes their notes<sup data-fn="410ea7f2-ae9e-4674-86f0-2800ce6db545" class="fn"><a href="#410ea7f2-ae9e-4674-86f0-2800ce6db545" id="410ea7f2-ae9e-4674-86f0-2800ce6db545-link">10</a></sup> – all without pausing the lecture.</p>

<h2 class="wp-block-heading"><strong>A Complete Ecosystem Designed To Take Skills and Creativity Further</strong></h2>



<p>The Galaxy Tab S12 series gives users the ideal place to put knowledge into practice, bringing together professional-grade apps<sup data-fn="dcfb54de-034b-47a4-98e1-aaf38958e755" class="fn"><a href="#dcfb54de-034b-47a4-98e1-aaf38958e755" id="dcfb54de-034b-47a4-98e1-aaf38958e755-link">14</a></sup> alongside benefits worth approximately $600<sup data-fn="02a832ed-aa3d-40bb-a1b8-66f5e66247a2" class="fn"><a href="#02a832ed-aa3d-40bb-a1b8-66f5e66247a2" id="02a832ed-aa3d-40bb-a1b8-66f5e66247a2-link">15</a></sup> to help them take their skills further.</p>



<p><strong>Adobe Photoshop</strong><sup data-fn="a0d2893a-f870-4e92-a529-e0446868e200" class="fn"><a href="#a0d2893a-f870-4e92-a529-e0446868e200" id="a0d2893a-f870-4e92-a529-e0446868e200-link">16</a></sup> comes pre-loaded on the Galaxy Tab S12 series — a first for Android tablets — bringing pro-grade image editing and design to the tablet. The interface is optimized for tablets, and S Pen support makes it more intuitive to move ideas from hand to screen. Photoshop includes AI-powered features including AI Assistant<sup data-fn="3a54d2c1-23ee-4a32-a1f7-20af32e40fa4" class="fn"><a href="#3a54d2c1-23ee-4a32-a1f7-20af32e40fa4" id="3a54d2c1-23ee-4a32-a1f7-20af32e40fa4-link">17</a></sup> and generative AI features powered by Adobe Firefly like Generative Fill and Image to Video, helping creatives edit faster and effortlessly. Exclusively for the Galaxy Tab S12 series, users receive four months of free access to the Photoshop mobile plan.</p>



<figure class="wp-block-image size-full"><img loading="lazy" width="1000" height="662" src="https://img.global.news.samsung.com/global/wp-content/uploads/2026/09/28112108/Samsung-Mobile-Galaxy-Tab-S12-Series_Main7.gif" alt="An image of Photoshop" class="wp-image-177473" /></figure>



<p><strong>Notability</strong>,<sup data-fn="78925e9e-42c3-413f-8bb2-e17a94490f83" class="fn"><a href="#78925e9e-42c3-413f-8bb2-e17a94490f83" id="78925e9e-42c3-413f-8bb2-e17a94490f83-link">18</a></sup> newly pre-loaded on Galaxy Tab, helps users capture their ideas, organize notes, and build knowledge more efficiently with S Pen optimization and AI-driven study tools with a six-month free trial, plus 20% off the first subscription.</p>



<figure class="wp-block-image size-full"><img loading="lazy" width="1000" height="662" src="https://img.global.news.samsung.com/global/wp-content/uploads/2026/09/28112317/Samsung-Mobile-Galaxy-Tab-S12-Series_Main8.gif" alt="An image of Notability " class="wp-image-177475" /></figure>



<p><strong>Goodnotes</strong><sup data-fn="09af2ec6-02a5-45eb-813e-118a4471a658" class="fn"><a href="#09af2ec6-02a5-45eb-813e-118a4471a658" id="09af2ec6-02a5-45eb-813e-118a4471a658-link">19</a></sup> delivers clean note-taking and organization with one year of free access and <strong>LumaFusion</strong><sup data-fn="2b59e311-ded9-42e2-a448-708ee4efc709" class="fn"><a href="#2b59e311-ded9-42e2-a448-708ee4efc709" id="2b59e311-ded9-42e2-a448-708ee4efc709-link">20</a></sup> enables professional-grade video editing, offered at a 66% discount. And <strong>Notion</strong><sup data-fn="7cb3413e-9123-4c56-888e-3e56a8d21502" class="fn"><a href="#7cb3413e-9123-4c56-888e-3e56a8d21502" id="7cb3413e-9123-4c56-888e-3e56a8d21502-link">21</a></sup> supports flexible writing, planning, and organization with one month of its Plus plan free. <strong>Clip Studio Paint</strong><sup data-fn="7a245eee-3e63-4998-a254-065c45c0bb1b" class="fn"><a href="#7a245eee-3e63-4998-a254-065c45c0bb1b" id="7a245eee-3e63-4998-a254-065c45c0bb1b-link">22</a></sup> brings precision drawing and painting tools for digital art with a six-month trial and 20% off the first subscription.</p>



<p>In addition to these apps, users can explore a broader range of options that support different needs. <strong>MyScript Math</strong> instantly solves handwritten equations and shows the steps behind each one, while <strong>MyScript Notes</strong> summarizes handwritten notes into study materials with built-in AI tools to explain concepts or generate quizzes. <strong>Noteshelf 3</strong><sup data-fn="143908d9-4250-4b29-ab2d-66d184adc649" class="fn"><a href="#143908d9-4250-4b29-ab2d-66d184adc649" id="143908d9-4250-4b29-ab2d-66d184adc649-link">23</a></sup> offers customizable layouts for capturing and organizing ideas, while <strong>ArcSite</strong><sup data-fn="8b32e9c4-c865-46d8-9608-5c880f5c709e" class="fn"><a href="#8b32e9c4-c865-46d8-9608-5c880f5c709e" id="8b32e9c4-c865-46d8-9608-5c880f5c709e-link">24</a></sup> converts rough field sketches into precise CAD drawings.</p>



<p></p>



<h2 class="wp-block-heading"><strong>Availability</strong></h2>



<p>The Galaxy Tab S12 series will be available starting Oct. 7 in select markets and offered in two colors, Gray and Silver.<sup data-fn="6ed65d9c-9f74-4e9e-aedd-44e1e24175f1" class="fn"><a href="#6ed65d9c-9f74-4e9e-aedd-44e1e24175f1" id="6ed65d9c-9f74-4e9e-aedd-44e1e24175f1-link">25</a></sup></p>



<p>Built for lasting and confident use, Galaxy Tab S12 Ultra and Galaxy Tab S12+ support up to seven times of OS upgrades and security updates.<sup data-fn="94681f80-5037-4c85-bb18-bb4f6c1fd370" class="fn"><a href="#94681f80-5037-4c85-bb18-bb4f6c1fd370" id="94681f80-5037-4c85-bb18-bb4f6c1fd370-link">26</a></sup></p>



<p>For added peace of mind, <strong>Samsung Care+</strong><sup data-fn="f448dc63-1544-4933-8196-51639d67a28b" class="fn"><a href="#f448dc63-1544-4933-8196-51639d67a28b" id="f448dc63-1544-4933-8196-51639d67a28b-link">27</a></sup> offers comprehensive coverage tailored to users’ device needs, including fast repairs for accidental damage, extended warranty, and certified expert support available both at home and abroad.  Additionally, Galaxy mobile phone users running One UI 9 can easily check the Samsung Care+ status of their Galaxy Tab and enroll through the Warranty and care menu in the Settings. It provides quick access to warranty information, self-diagnostics, repair requests, and remote support for connected devices—all directly from the mobile phone.</p>



<p>For additional information, visit <a href="https://news.samsung.com/global/" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer noopener">Samsung Newsroom</a>, <a href="https://www.samsungmobilepress.com/" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer noopener">SamsungMobilePress.com</a>, or <a href="https://www.samsung.com/global/galaxy/" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer noopener">Samsung.com</a>.</p>

Launched in June 2026, Helix is an AI-enabling infrastructure provider that is designed to […] <p>Samsung Electronics, Samsung C&T, Samsung SDS, Samsung SDI, Samsung Life Insurance and Samsung Fire & Marine Insurance today announced they are investing a combined USD 1 billion in Helix Digital Infrastructure, an AI infrastructure company established by global investment firm KKR.</p>



<p>Launched in June 2026, Helix is an AI-enabling infrastructure provider that is designed to deliver integrated solutions across the entire value chain, including hyperscale data center development and operations, power generation (covering both baseload and flexible energy sources), transmission and distribution infrastructure, and fiber-optic networks.</p>



<p>Through Helix, hyperscalers can rapidly secure the large-scale, comprehensive infrastructure required to keep pace with exponentially growing AI demands.</p>



<p>Helix is led by Adam Selipsky, former CEO of Amazon Web Services (AWS) and is assembling a dedicated management team comprising leading experts from the data center and power industries. Helix also leverages the capabilities of KKR’s leading global infrastructure business with about 170 dedicated professionals.</p>



<p>Alongside Samsung, KKR, the Kuwait Investment Authority (KIA), NVIDIA, and US power company Vistra are also founding investors in Helix.</p>



<p></p>



<h2 class="wp-block-heading"><strong>AI Data Centers Evolve Into Core Platforms for a New Industrial Ecosystem</strong></h2>



<p>AI data centers are rapidly evolving beyond simple facility investments into core business platforms. Today, they integrate diverse, high-value business models, including GPU-as-a-Service (GPUaaS), Sovereign AI, and colocation services.</p>



<p>The rapid expansion of these data centers is driving the creation of a massive, multi-sector ecosystem that spans power generation, cooling technologies, semiconductors, and finance. Samsung affiliates’ investment reflects a strategic commitment to securing next-generation AI leadership within this emerging infrastructure landscape.</p>



<p>As AI services integrate deeper into daily life and global industries, the need for data centers capable of processing massive compute workloads — and the specialized power infrastructure required to run them stably — is growing exponentially. If data centers are the production hubs of the AI era, power is the fuel that drives them. Consequently, the ability to secure both compute and power infrastructure simultaneously has emerged as a defining competitive advantage in the AI industry.</p>



<p>By targeting both data center deployment and the underlying power infrastructure, Helix’s investment strategy addresses the very core of this market competition. Recognizing that power availability is currently the greatest bottleneck in AI infrastructure, Helix plans to rapidly secure energy capacity through direct investments and strategic partnerships with major energy developers, including investor Vistra.</p>



<p>The investment this time is expected to serve as a pivotal milestone for Samsung’s affiliates, allowing them to leverage their world-class hardware and component expertise to build system- and infrastructure-level influence. Moving forward, Samsung is poised to expand its role from a traditional hardware component supplier to an active architect of the global AI infrastructure ecosystem.</p>



<p></p>



<h2 class="wp-block-heading"><strong>Samsung’s Expertise Across the AI Infrastructure Stack Serves as Critical Anchor for Global AI Buildout</strong></h2>



<p>Samsung Electronics’ Device Solutions (DS) Division supports the global buildout with its state-of-the-art semiconductor solutions, responding to changes in global demand in a timely manner.</p>



<p>Samsung Electronics’ Device eXperience (DX) Division delivers a comprehensive portfolio of data center cooling products and solutions — from air cooling systems to coolant distribution units (CDUs) for liquid cooling — through FläktGroup, its data center HVAC specialist subsidiary acquired in 2025. It also operates 14 production sites worldwide and a supply and service network across 65 countries, giving it the speed and flexibility to meet hyperscalers’ demands for rapid capacity expansion.</p>



<p>Samsung C&T Engineering & Construction Group is actively driving the large-scale infrastructure projects as an engineering, procurement, and construction (EPC) contractor across data centers and power generation.</p>



<p>Samsung SDS is an IT solutions provider that also designs, builds and operates data centers and has recently ventured into the GPUaaS business. The company boasts the lowest power usage effectiveness (PUE) level in Korea which it applies both to its own data centers and to the Korea AI Computing Center (KOACC).</p>



<p>Samsung SDI is recognized for world-leading technology in uninterruptible power supplies (UPS) and battery backup units (BBUs), both essential to running data centers that operate high-performance servers 24 hours a day. It plans to explore new business opportunities in this field, where ultra-high-output, high-capacity battery technology is essential.</p>



<p>Through this investment, Samsung Electronics and its affiliates aim to accelerate global AI data center deployment alongside Helix, while maximizing collaborative synergies among participating companies.</p> [World Heart Day] Taking Care of Your Heart Every Day, Starting From Your Wrist https://news.samsung.com/global/world-heart-day-taking-care-of-your-heart-every-day-starting-from-your-wrist Tue, 29 Sep 2026 08:00:00 +0000 Samsung Newsroom Mobile Galaxy Watch Heart Health Samsung Health World Heart Day https://bit.ly/3VTo0u9 No matter who you are or what you do, we all have one constant companion we rely on every single day: our heart. Unseen but always at work, it beats around 100,000 times a day, keeping pace with us through every moment. Yet for something so essential to our lives, taking care of our hearts […] <p>No matter who you are or what you do, we all have one constant companion we rely on every single day: our heart. Unseen but always at work, it beats around 100,000 times a day, keeping pace with us through every moment. Yet for something so essential to our lives, taking care of our hearts is often overlooked until it’s too late.</p>



<p>Heart disease is the number one cause of death worldwide,<sup data-fn="f2e7e42b-e6ef-4fa1-a6a0-c9a019583e81" class="fn"><a href="#f2e7e42b-e6ef-4fa1-a6a0-c9a019583e81" id="f2e7e42b-e6ef-4fa1-a6a0-c9a019583e81-link">1</a></sup> which is why the World Health Organization (WHO) and the World Heart Federation recognize Sept. 29 as <a href="https://world-heart-federation.org/world-heart-day/" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer noopener">World Heart Day</a>. It is a reminder that the choices we make today can make a meaningful difference tomorrow.</p>



<p>These choices don’t have to be big. From choosing when to go to bed to making time for even just 30 minutes of exercise, the small choices we make every day add up to a healthier lifestyle. The good news is that Samsung Galaxy Watch can help you make proactive prevention part of daily life. By monitoring your sleep, activity level, stress, and blood pressure, Galaxy Watch helps you better understand what’s happening in your body and what steps to take to ensure your heart is doing its job well.</p>



<p></p>



<h2 class="wp-block-heading">Understanding Your Body at a Glance: Heart Health Score</h2>



<p>The American Heart Association recommends looking at <a href="https://www.heart.org/en/healthy-living/healthy-lifestyle/lifes-essential-8" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer noopener">Life’s Essential 8</a><sup data-fn="d9aa41a5-f411-46c8-8dfb-89f468e36fcc" class="fn"><a href="#d9aa41a5-f411-46c8-8dfb-89f468e36fcc" id="d9aa41a5-f411-46c8-8dfb-89f468e36fcc-link">2</a></sup> as key measures for improving and maintaining cardiovascular health: diet, exercise, sleep, weight, blood pressure, blood sugar, cholesterol and smoking cessation. But knowing what matters raises an important question: how do these factors change the choices we make every day?</p>



<p>Samsung Health’s <strong>Heart Health Score</strong> is designed to help you in this journey. It tracks and analyzes complex health indicators such as sleep duration, daily activity level, body composition and vascular load,<sup data-fn="6fe3477b-d357-420b-a815-55482b7c5a12" class="fn"><a href="#6fe3477b-d357-420b-a815-55482b7c5a12" id="6fe3477b-d357-420b-a815-55482b7c5a12-link">3</a></sup> converting them into a single, simple score that anyone can intuitively understand and follow.</p>



<p>Think about what happens if you miss your daily exercise for a few days: your Galaxy Watch recognizes your moderate-to-high intensity activity level has decreased, reflects this change in a lower Heart Health Score, and makes a recommendation to go for a run. With convenient insights right from your wrist, Galaxy Watch delivers personalized insights that make it easier to recognize changes, understand your habits, and take the next step toward healthier ones.</p>
