The announcement of the partnership was made during the state summit held in Paris between South Korea and France. Under the agreement, Samsung will […] <p>Samsung Electronics today announced it has entered into a strategic partnership with Mistral AI to enhance its semiconductor engineering and manufacturing capabilities that will reinforce its leadership in AI chip technologies.</p>



<p>The announcement of the partnership was made during the state summit held in Paris between South Korea and France. Under the agreement, Samsung will integrate Mistral’s AI services and solutions — including its flagship large language model, Mistral Large — across its semiconductor operations to develop customized on-premises AI models optimized for intelligence-driven infrastructure.</p>



<p>Mistral’s powerful AI platform is expected to provide Samsung with a unique stack of tools that will transform how semiconductors are designed and manufactured.</p>



<p>The on-premises enterprise solutions will especially ensure security and flexibility required to process highly sensitive technologies and operational data entirely within the boundaries of Samsung’s semiconductor infrastructure, maintaining total control over its mission-critical technologies.</p>



<p>As semiconductor processes become more advanced and complex, rapid data analysis in the fab is essential. By applying targeted AI models to defect detection and equipment optimization, Samsung aims to accelerate development cycles, manufacturing precision, and yield stabilization across its advanced memory and logic chips.</p>



<p>“Increasing complexities involved in AI chip design and manufacturing requires continuous innovation in semiconductor technologies,” said Young Hyun Jun, Vice Chairman & CEO of the Device Solutions (DS) Division at Samsung Electronics. “We look forward to working with Mistral and support the evolving needs of customers, while delivering new breakthroughs across the semiconductor ecosystem essential to the AI era.”</p>



<p>“AI is reshaping how we build complex technologies, from silicon to software,” said Arthur Mensch, co-founder and CEO of Mistral. “We are proud to support Samsung Electronics with our expertise in electronics and semiconductors, helping to improve how chips are designed and manufactured, and to accelerate technical progress across the global semiconductor and AI value chain.”</p>



<p>Samsung has also led Mistral AI’s Series D funding round, securing a strategic equity stake to support long-term technology collaboration. Samsung’s lead investment reflects expanding cross-industry collaboration across the advanced chip ecosystem.</p>



<p>By joining its leading silicon technologies with AI innovators, such as Mistral, Samsung plans to continue its progress toward a broader AI transformation across its semiconductor operations and meet the diverse needs of its customers spanning advanced memory and logic to foundry services.</p> From 253 Grams to 201 Grams: The Engineering Behind the World’s Lightest Fold https://news.samsung.com/global/from-253-grams-to-201-grams-the-engineering-behind-the-worlds-lightest-fold Wed, 09 Sep 2026 01:00:00 +0000 Samsung Newsroom Mobile Foldable Galaxy Z Fold Hardware https://bit.ly/4iSJBwh Since introducing the first Galaxy Fold at 276 grams in 2019, Samsung Electronics has continued to expand what a foldable smartphone can be, advancing hinge and display design, durability, and usability with every generation. As those technologies evolved, Samsung focused on another challenge: making a large-screen foldable easier to carry every day, without losing the […] <p>Since introducing the first Galaxy Fold at 276 grams in 2019, Samsung Electronics has continued to expand what a foldable smartphone can be, advancing hinge and display design, durability, and usability with every generation.</p>



<p>As those technologies evolved, Samsung focused on another challenge: making a large-screen foldable easier to carry every day, without losing the experiences that make the form factor unique.</p>



<p>And through that pursuit, Galaxy Z Fold5 weighed 253 grams, and Galaxy Z Fold6 reduced its weight further to 239 grams.</p>



<p>With Galaxy Z Fold7 came an even more ambitious goal:</p>



<p class="has-text-align-left">“Could a foldable be lighter than a bar-type flagship smartphone?”</p>



<p>At the time, Galaxy S25 Ultra — one of Samsung’s flagship bar-type smartphones — weighed 218 grams. So, the development team set an even lighter weight target for Galaxy Z Fold7: 215 grams.</p>



<p>Galaxy Z Fold7 reached that goal. And now, building on years of foldable innovation, Galaxy Z Fold8 combines a new form factor with advances in core technologies, materials, and internal architecture to weigh just 201 grams — 52 grams lighter than Galaxy Z Fold5. At 201 grams, Galaxy Z Fold8 is the world’s lightest fold.<sup data-fn="b3039eda-75fd-4d2a-bf5a-44873bc4288d" class="fn"><a href="#b3039eda-75fd-4d2a-bf5a-44873bc4288d" id="b3039eda-75fd-4d2a-bf5a-44873bc4288d-link">1</a></sup></p>



<figure class="wp-block-image size-full"><img loading="lazy" width="1000" height="563" src="https://img.global.news.samsung.com/global/wp-content/uploads/2026/09/08193810/Samsung-Mobile-Engineering-Behind-Fold_Main1.jpg" alt="Galaxy Z Fold’s weight and thickness by generation" class="wp-image-177277" /><figcaption class="wp-element-caption">▲ Galaxy Z Fold’s weight and thickness by generation</figcaption></figure>



<p>But consumers expected more than just a lighter foldable.</p>



<p>A <a href="https://yougov.com/en-us/articles/50945-foldable-phones-high-interest-among-young-adults-durability-a-major-concern" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer noopener">2024 YouGov survey</a><sup data-fn="2ab6cffd-70e3-48cf-8ed3-d80151fc3a6b" class="fn"><a href="#2ab6cffd-70e3-48cf-8ed3-d80151fc3a6b" id="2ab6cffd-70e3-48cf-8ed3-d80151fc3a6b-link">2</a></sup> found that a bulky or heavy design remained a concern among consumers not planning to purchase foldables, while battery life, durability, and display quality ranked as top priorities for those considering them.</p>



<p>This insight shaped a core engineering goal for Samsung: make foldables thinner and lighter, while maintaining the core experiences users value most.</p>



<p>Battery life, camera performance, thermal management, and durability still had to advance.</p>



<p>That could not be achieved by changing a single component. Samsung engineers had to reimagine the entire device — advancing fundamental foldable technologies, introducing new materials, and revisiting hundreds of components and auxiliary parts to the fraction-of-a-gram level.</p>



<p>From 253 grams to 201 grams. Three engineering innovations enabled that transformation.</p>



<figure class="wp-block-video"><video playsinline autoplay controls loop muted src="https://img.global.news.samsung.com/global/wp-content/uploads/2026/09/08173710/Samsung-Mobile-Engineering-Behind-Fold_Main2.mp4"></video><figcaption class="wp-element-caption">▲ <em>From Galaxy Z Fold5 to Galaxy Z Fold8, Samsung reduced 52 grams to deliver the world’s lightest foldable.</em></figcaption></figure>



<p></p>



<h2 class="wp-block-heading">The Thinner and Lighter a Foldable Gets, the More Precise the Engineering Must Become</h2>



<p>Making a foldable thinner and lighter brings a new set of engineering challenges.</p>



<p>The layers inside the display need to become thinner while still meeting Samsung’s durability and reliability requirements. The metal plate supporting the display needs to stay extremely flat even as it becomes thinner. And the hinge has to manage the forces created every time the device folds and unfolds, while being designed around the size and structure of each product.</p>



<p>Across generations, Samsung has continued to advance the display layers, ultra-thin metal precision machining, and hinge technologies central to its foldable devices.</p>



<p>One example is <a href="https://news.samsung.com/global/samsung-introduces-flex-titanium-technology-to-advance-foldable-displays" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer noopener">Flex Titanium</a>.</p>



<p>The displays in Galaxy Z Fold8 and Galaxy Z Fold8 Ultra use Flex Titanium technology featuring a Titanium Alloy Film designed for the foldable display structure.</p>



<p>The film is just tens of micrometers thick — roughly one-third the width of a human hair. Its material and structure work together to maintain performance at this extreme thickness.</p>



<p>The metal plate that supports the display presents a different challenge.</p>



<p>Some metal plates are machined to approximately 0.2 millimeters thick, with the thinnest sections reaching 0.15 millimeters. At that level, simply making the metal thinner is not enough. Thin metal can bend or warp more easily during machining, and even a small change in shape can affect assembly precision or the surface quality of the display.</p>



<p>What matters is the ability to machine ultra-thin metal at around 0.2 millimeters while consistently achieving the required shape and flatness.</p>



<p>Samsung refined the machining sequence, tooling, cooling conditions, and process parameters, breaking the process into multiple stages to maintain precision.</p>



<p>The hinge requires the same level of care.</p>



<p>Beyond simply opening and closing the phone, it must distribute the forces created by repeated folding and unfolding across the device while maintaining structural stability.</p>



<p>And these requirements vary by product size and architecture.</p>



<p>Samsung engineers the hinge for each form factor, carefully balancing load distribution, strength, durability, internal space, and weight rather than just scaling it down from one generation to the next.</p>



<p>Creating a thinner and lighter foldable requires more than downsizing individual parts.</p>



<p>It comes from greater precision in how materials are machined, how forces are managed, and how structures are designed within a limited space.</p>



<p></p>



<h2 class="wp-block-heading">Lighter, Built for Strength — Starting With the Materials</h2>



<p>The second area is materials innovation.</p>



<p>Different parts of a foldable have very different jobs. Some demand high rigidity. Some must withstand repeated movement. Others need to spread heat efficiently.</p>



<p>Samsung selects and develops materials around those specific needs, delivering the performance each requires while optimizing weight and space.</p>



<p>In Galaxy Z Fold8 and Galaxy Z Fold8 Ultra, Flex Titanium incorporates a Titanium Alloy Film to support the foldable display structure.</p>



<p>For the exterior, Samsung uses Advanced Armor Aluminum, helping provide the strength and durability needed in a thin and lightweight frame.</p>



<p>Materials also play an important role in thermal management.</p>



<p>As a device gets thinner, there is less room to manage the heat generated inside. Samsung uses graphite with improved thermal performance, while tailoring the overall thermal structure to the size and performance requirements of each product.</p>



<p>In Galaxy Z Fold8 Ultra, which has similar overall dimensions to Galaxy Z Fold7, the volume of the graphite heat-dissipation layer was increased by approximately 7% to help support its enhanced performance.</p>



<p>Samsung also applies Clad Metal inside the rear of the device. By combining the characteristics of different metals, the material helps reduce weight while providing the thermal performance required within a thin structure.</p>



<p>Materials innovation is therefore not just about choosing lighter options, but about using the right material in the right place to deliver the performance each part needs with less weight and space.</p>



<p></p>



<h2 class="wp-block-heading">Looking Again at the Entire Device — Down to 0.001 Grams</h2>



<p>Only so much can be achieved through core technologies and new materials alone.</p>



<p>In a foldable already packed with highly integrated components, finding the next few grams means looking again at almost everything inside the device.</p>



<p>Samsung engineers reviewed approximately 270 components and more than 180 types of auxiliary parts.</p>



<p>This spanned everything from the display, hinge, and cameras to circuit boards, antennas, brackets, tapes, and fasteners.</p>



<p>The precision extended to reductions of 0.1 grams and 0.1 millimeters, and in some auxiliary parts, as little as 0.001 grams.</p>



<p>The printed circuit board, or PCB, was redesigned to remove unnecessary circuits, improve component layout, and minimize its footprint.</p>



<p>The MFC (Magnetic Flux Channel) antenna was also reimagined to reduce unnecessary overlap and interference with surrounding components, allowing the limited internal space to be used more efficiently.</p>



<p>Every auxiliary part, no matter how small, was examined.</p>



<p>Of the more than 180 types previously used, approximately 15 were eliminated.</p>



<p>Engineers then reviewed the remaining 170 types, trimming oversized areas, refining shapes, and optimizing dimensions. Multiple parts were consolidated into single structures where possible.</p>



<p>In some cases, the savings were measured in increments as small as 0.001 grams.</p>



<p>Each change seems minor in isolation, but repeated across the device, fractions of a gram become tenths of a gram, then whole grams.</p>



<p>The weight savings on a finished device result from hundreds of small engineering decisions.</p>



<p></p>



<h2 class="wp-block-heading">Finding 6 Grams Again To Make Room for More</h2>



<p>The goal was to reinvest the weight and space recovered through engineering into the capabilities people actually experience — including the battery, cameras, and thermal system.</p>



<p>Galaxy Z Fold8 Ultra provides a separate example of how this approach can be applied.</p>



<p>Galaxy Z Fold8 Ultra and Galaxy Z Fold7 have similar overall dimensions and both weigh 215 grams. But within that same weight, Galaxy Z Fold8 Ultra increases battery capacity from 4,400 mAh to 5,000 mAh.</p>



<p>That larger battery brought its own challenge: the battery itself added approximately 6 grams.</p>



<p>So the development team faced a new question:</p>



<p class="has-text-align-left">“Where could we find those 6 grams again?”</p>



<p>There was no single component that could provide the answer.</p>



<p>Instead, engineers applied the same systematic approach across the entire device — materials and structures, circuit layouts, and component placement, down to the smallest auxiliary parts — identifying weight savings across all components.</p>



<p>This strategy extended to internal space optimization.</p>



<p>By reducing unnecessary overlap between the display, circuits, antennas, and surrounding components, and by improving packaging efficiency, Samsung created room for a larger battery and enhanced thermal and camera systems.</p>



<p>As a result, Galaxy Z Fold8 Ultra accommodates a 5,000 mAh battery while increasing the volume of its graphite heat-dissipation layer by approximately 7% compared with the similarly sized Galaxy Z Fold7.</p>



<p>Galaxy Z Fold8 Ultra features a 200 MP main camera and introduces the first 50 MP Ultra-Wide camera on a Galaxy Z Fold device.</p>



<p>Samsung’s approach to a thinner and lighter foldable is therefore not about removing capabilities.</p>



<p>It is about redirecting weight and space savings toward the flagship experiences users value most.</p>



<p></p>



<h2 class="wp-block-heading">From 253 Grams to 201 Grams: Redefining the Weight Standard for Foldables</h2>



<p>The journey began with the first Galaxy Fold at 276 grams in 2019. Across eight generations, Samsung has continued to advance the technologies, materials, and internal architecture behind the foldable experience.</p>



<p>In recent generations, that progress accelerated — from 253 grams with Galaxy Z Fold5 to 201 grams with Galaxy Z Fold8.</p>



<p>But the story isn’t just about reducing weight.</p>



<p>Samsung’s goal has been to make a large-screen foldable easier to carry without giving up the qualities users expect from a flagship smartphone.</p>



<p>The weight and space recovered through advances in core technologies, materials, and component design were put back into the battery, cameras, thermal system, and other flagship experiences users value most.</p>



<p>From 253 grams to 201 grams.</p>



<p>No single material or component made that transformation possible.</p>



<p>Eight generations of engineering enabled this, advancing core technologies, introducing new materials, and rethinking the entire device to make Galaxy Z Fold easier to carry without compromising the flagship experience.</p>



<p><strong><strong>Galaxy Z Fold Series: A Thinner, Lighter Journey in Numbers</strong></strong></p>



<p>– 52 g<br>Galaxy Z Fold8 is 52 grams lighter than Galaxy Z Fold5.</p>



<p>1/3, 0.2 mm<br>The Titanium Alloy Film used in Flex Titanium is approximately one-third the width of a human hair, while ultra-thin metal structures supporting the display are precision-machined to around 0.2 millimeters.</p>



<p>~270 / 180+ / 0.001 g<br>Samsung re-examined approximately 270 components and more than 180 types of auxiliary parts, optimizing some down to 0.001 grams.</p>



<p>5,000 mAh / +~7% / 200 MP + 50 MP<br>Galaxy Z Fold8 Ultra features a 5,000 mAh battery, approximately 7% more graphite heat-dissipation volume and a 200 MP main camera with a 50 MP Ultra-Wide camera.</p>

Building on years of successful cooperation, the companies will work together to accelerate the development and deployment […] <p>Samsung Electronics and ASML today announced an expansion of their long-standing strategic partnership, deepening collaboration on High NA EUV lithography and advanced semiconductor manufacturing technologies that will help drive the next generation of innovation in the AI era.</p>



<p>Building on years of successful cooperation, the companies will work together to accelerate the development and deployment of technologies that support the increasing performance and efficiency requirements of advanced semiconductor manufacturing.</p>



<p>Samsung will join the industry initiative to advance next-generation 12-inch photomask technology for future manufacturing. The semiconductor industry has relied on the 6-inch photomask format for decades. With High NA EUV, the transition to larger, 12-inch masks is expected to further increase fab productivity, lower chipmaking costs and remove stitching constraints. It will enable advanced chip manufacturers to fully leverage the advantages of High NA EUV, making future generations of leading-edge chips more cost-effective.</p>



<p>Drawing on its leadership in both memory and foundry manufacturing, as well as its extensive experience with advanced EUV lithography, Samsung is positioned to play a critical role in the 12-inch photomask transition. The company will work closely with industry partners to develop the required mask technologies and supporting infrastructure.</p>



<p>Samsung also plans to introduce ASML’s High NA extreme ultraviolet (EUV) lithography into future DRAM high-volume manufacturing for the first time in the industry by 2028, further demonstrating the readiness of High NA technology to support advanced memory and logic applications. High NA EUV is expected to extend the DRAM scaling roadmap, with improved resolution enabling process simplification and increased efficiency.</p>



<p>The announcement reflects a shared commitment to innovation, technology leadership, and long-term partnership. Samsung and ASML expect to continue exploring opportunities for collaboration in areas including advanced memory and innovative manufacturing approaches.</p>



<p>Young Hyun Jun, Vice Chairman & CEO, Samsung Electronics, said: “The AI era is transforming the semiconductor industry and increasing the importance of technological innovation across the entire value chain. Samsung is committed to maintaining leadership in advanced semiconductor manufacturing, including memory and foundry, through breakthrough technologies and strong partnerships. By further strengthening our collaboration with ASML, we are helping lay the foundation for the next generation of AI and semiconductor innovation.”</p>



<p>Christophe Fouquet, President and Chief Executive Officer, ASML, said: “Samsung has been one of ASML’s most important innovation partners for many years. Together, we have helped advance the technologies that underpin modern semiconductor manufacturing. As the industry enters a new era driven by artificial intelligence, close collaboration with our customers becomes even more important. We look forward to working with Samsung to enable the next wave of semiconductor innovation.”</p>



<hr class="wp-block-separator has-alpha-channel-opacity" />



<h4 class="wp-block-heading"><strong>About ASML Holding N.V.</strong><br>ASML is a leading supplier to the semiconductor industry. The company provides chipmakers with hardware, software and services to mass produce the patterns of integrated circuits (microchips). Together with its partners, ASML drives the advancement of more affordable, more powerful, more energy-efficient microchips. ASML enables groundbreaking technology to solve some of humanity’s toughest challenges, such as in healthcare, energy use and conservation, mobility, and agriculture. ASML is a multinational company headquartered in Veldhoven, the Netherlands, with offices across EMEA, the US and Asia. Every day, ASML’s more than 44,000 employees (FTE) challenge the status quo and push technology to new limits. ASML is traded on Euronext Amsterdam and NASDAQ under the symbol ASML. Discover ASML — our products, technology and career opportunities — at <a href="https://www.asml.com/en/" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer noopener">www.asml.com</a>.</h4> Immaculate Engineering Meets Artistic Imagination: Highlights From Design Miami Seoul 2026 https://news.samsung.com/global/immaculate-engineering-meets-artistic-imagination-highlights-from-design-miami-seoul-2026 Mon, 07 Sep 2026 14:53:00 +0000 Samsung Newsroom Design Design is an Act of Love Design Miami Seoul 2026 OLED S95H Samsung Design https://bit.ly/4yhBGx4 What happens when human imagination, artificial intelligence, art and technology come together?  From September 1 to 6, Samsung Electronics presented a special exhibition themed “Design is an Act of Love” during Design Miami Seoul 2026 at Dongdaemun Design Plaza (DDP) in Seoul. The exhibition offered a fresh interpretation of Samsung’s human-centered design vision. Inspired by […] <p>What happens when human imagination, artificial intelligence, art and technology come together?  From September 1 to 6, Samsung Electronics presented a special exhibition themed “Design is an Act of Love” during Design Miami Seoul 2026 at Dongdaemun Design Plaza (DDP) in Seoul.</p>



<p>The exhibition offered a fresh interpretation of Samsung’s human-centered design vision. Inspired by Samsung products, 14 Korean artists drew on their sensibilities and imagination to interpret an act of love in their own ways. In the process, some used AI to develop their ideas and expand the possibilities of creative expression. Visitors viewed the completed works on OLED TV S95H displays, each featuring a custom frame with distinct materials and forms.</p>



<p>Samsung Newsroom visited “Design is an Act of Love” to see how art, technology, and design came together.</p>



<figure class="wp-block-image size-full"><img loading="lazy" width="1000" height="580" src="https://img.global.news.samsung.com/global/wp-content/uploads/2026/09/07102339/Samsung-Corporate-Design-Miami-Seoul-2026-Design-is-an-Act-of-Love-Sketch_main1.jpg" alt="Visitors explore Samsung’s exhibition at Design Miami Seoul 2026." class="wp-image-177126" /><figcaption class="wp-element-caption">▲ Visitors explore Samsung’s exhibition at Design Miami Seoul 2026.</figcaption></figure>



<p></p>



<h2 class="wp-block-heading"><strong>14 Acts of Love Inspired by Samsung Products</strong></h2>



<p>Upon entering, visitors were greeted by 14 artworks displayed on OLED TV S95H screens. Moving through the space, they encountered distinct colors, compositions, and narratives — each offering a different interpretation of a Samsung product.</p>



<p>Beyond the individual artworks, the exhibition explored the evolving relationship between human creativity and artificial intelligence, expressed through the formula AI × (EI + HI) — Artificial Intelligence amplified by Emotional Intelligence and Human Imagination.</p>



<p>The artworks began with human intuition, emotion, point of view, and imagination. Some artists then entered into a dialogue with AI, using it not to replace their creativity, but to expand it, animate it, and take it into unexpected new territories. Technology amplifies the human. It does not replace it.</p>



<figure class="wp-block-image size-full"><img loading="lazy" width="1000" height="509" src="https://img.global.news.samsung.com/global/wp-content/uploads/2026/09/07102438/Samsung-Corporate-Design-Miami-Seoul-2026-Design-is-an-Act-of-Love-Sketch_main2.jpg" alt="The works were inspired by 14 Samsung products spanning mobile devices, TVs, and home appliances." class="wp-image-177127" /><figcaption class="wp-element-caption">▲ The works were inspired by 14 Samsung products spanning mobile devices, TVs, and home appliances.</figcaption></figure>



<p>Aihyung Jo’s “EVERYTHING, SO FAR…” draws inspiration from Galaxy Z Fold8, using metaphor and humor to explore the human desire to express one’s feelings to others.</p>



<p>Across the foldable’s inner and outer displays, visual symbols of human progress illustrate how technology has expanded human capabilities and opened countless possibilities.</p>

<p>Inspired by Music Studio 5, the work visualizes sound not simply as something to be heard but as a shared experience. Waveforms radiating from the center and expanding layers show how a single sound fills a space, bringing together those within it.</p>

<h4 class="wp-block-heading has-text-align-center">▲ Sukeee discusses the inspiration behind “The Aural Sensation.”</h4>



<p>While their approaches differed, human experience and emotion remained at the heart of each work — with some artists incorporating AI into their creative process.</p>



<p></p>



<h2 class="wp-block-heading"><strong>One Display, 14 Distinct Expressions</strong></h2>



<p>A key part of the showcase was the custom frames surrounding the artworks. Rather than following a single style, the 14 frames created for OLED TV S95H featured distinct materials, forms, and finishes. Each gave the same display a different character.</p>



<p>The frames also reflect the exhibition’s exploration of expression. After years in which technology has increasingly gravitated toward neutral, minimal, and universal forms, the exhibition explored a different possibility: bringing greater diversity, personality, energy, and emotion into the objects that surround us, and allowing technology to connect more deeply with individual identity.</p>



<p>For “Spring Carries Love,” Guimi Yoo reimagines The Frame Pro as a bridge between viewers and the world within the artwork. The piece was presented in a mint-colored acrylic frame with a hazy finish.</p>

<h4 class="wp-block-heading has-text-align-center">▲ “Spring Carries Love” by Guimi Yoo</h4>



<p>Inspired by the Infinite AI Air Purifier, “Picnic of May” by design studio Kiljong Arcade depicts a peaceful everyday moment. The work was displayed within a blue lattice frame combining matte and glossy finishes.</p>



<div class="wp-block-create-block-image-slider-normal"><div><div>
				<div class="fixed-box-slider">
					<div class="gallery-inner">
						<ul>

<h4 class="wp-block-heading has-text-align-center">▲ “Picnic of May” by Kiljong Arcade</h4>



<p>“Axis of Time, Terrain of Memory” presents Galaxy Watch9 and Galaxy Watch Ultra2 as companions that document users’ daily lives.</p>



<p>“I visualized the various types of everyday data accumulated on Galaxy Watch through changes in typography, color, lines, and density,” said Byungrok Chae, the artist behind “Axis of Time, Terrain of Memory.” “I extended the pixel structure of the digital display into the custom frame so the screen and frame flow together as a single graphic. This helped bring out the meaning and visual characteristics of Galaxy Watch.”</p>

<h4 class="wp-block-heading has-text-align-center">▲ Chae explains the concept behind “Axis of Time, Terrain of Memory.”</h4>



<p>Samsung’s OLED TV S95H became a canvas for this exploration. Its Float Layer Design makes the display appear to float above a real metal plate, and for Design Miami Seoul, this concept was taken further through 14 unique custom frames, each created in dialogue with the artwork displayed on screen.</p>



<p>By incorporating its structure and materials as design elements, the TV itself becomes a design object within the space. Paired with 14 custom frames, the display showed how the same product could take on different expressions depending on its surroundings and individual tastes, bringing Samsung’s Expressive Design philosophy to life.</p>



<p></p>



<h2 class="wp-block-heading"><strong>Human-Centered Design in the AI Era</strong></h2>



<figure class="wp-block-image size-full"><img loading="lazy" width="1000" height="680" src="https://img.global.news.samsung.com/global/wp-content/uploads/2026/09/07144911/Samsung-Corporate-Design-Miami-Seoul-2026-Design-is-an-Act-of-Love-Sketch_main17.jpg" alt="Porcini at Design Miami Seoul 2026" class="wp-image-177170" /><figcaption class="wp-element-caption">▲ Porcini at Design Miami Seoul 2026</figcaption></figure>



<p>During the exhibition, Samsung hosted a design seminar titled “The Language of Creativity,” where Mauro Porcini, President and Chief Design Officer (CDO) of the Device eXperience (DX) Division at Samsung Electronics, discussed the company’s human-centered design vision and the role of design in the AI era.</p>



<p>Porcini emphasized that technology should bring warmth and value to people’s lives, while AI can expand human empathy and imagination.</p>



<figure class="wp-block-image size-full"><img loading="lazy" width="1000" height="719" src="https://img.global.news.samsung.com/global/wp-content/uploads/2026/09/07144931/Samsung-Corporate-Design-Miami-Seoul-2026-Design-is-an-Act-of-Love-Sketch_main18.jpg" alt="Porcini shares Samsung’s design vision." class="wp-image-177171" /><figcaption class="wp-element-caption">▲ Porcini shares Samsung’s design vision.</figcaption></figure>



<p>“This exhibition stems from our belief that ‘Design is an Act of Love,’” said Porcini. “Through it, we hope to show how technology can enrich what makes us human.”</p>



<p>Together, these ideas offered a broader interpretation of “Design is an Act of Love”: understanding people deeply enough to celebrate their differences, amplify their imagination, and use technology to create more human, diverse, and expressive possibilities.</p>



<p>At Design Miami Seoul 2026, Samsung demonstrated how its human-centered design vision extends across products and spaces when paired with artists’ imaginations. As the AI era continues to unfold, the company remains focused on design that understands and cares for people.</p> Samsung Medison Expands Women’s Health Diagnostic Portfolio With Launch of Premium Ultrasound ‘HERA Z10’ https://news.samsung.com/global/samsung-medison-expands-womens-health-diagnostic-portfolio-with-launch-of-premium-ultrasound-hera-z10 Mon, 07 Sep 2026 09:00:00 +0000 Samsung Newsroom More Products Press Releases HERA Z Series HERA Z10 ISUOG Samsung Medison Ultrasound System https://bit.ly/4yiYYCL Samsung Medison launched its premium obstetrics and gynecology (OB/GYN) ultrasound diagnostic system, the HERA Z10. Alongside the flagship HERA Z20, the new system officially completes the HERA Z Series (Z20 & Z10) lineup. With global trends showing an increase in maternal age and pregnancy-related complications, precision imaging for prenatal diagnostics and rapid clinical decision-making have […] <p>Samsung Medison launched its premium obstetrics and gynecology (OB/GYN) ultrasound diagnostic system, the HERA Z10. Alongside the flagship HERA Z20, the new system officially completes the HERA Z Series (Z20 & Z10) lineup.</p>



<figure class="wp-block-image size-full"><img loading="lazy" width="1000" height="700" src="https://img.global.news.samsung.com/global/wp-content/uploads/2026/09/07082959/Samsung-More-Products-Samsung-Medison-HERA-Z10_Main1.jpg" alt=" HERA Z Series (Z20 & Z10) lineup" class="wp-image-177116" /></figure>



<p>With global trends showing an increase in maternal age and pregnancy-related complications, precision imaging for prenatal diagnostics and rapid clinical decision-making have become increasingly vital. As a result, women’s health diagnostic systems are evolving beyond basic fetal evaluations to encompass early complication prediction, maternal health assessments, and holistic wellness.</p>



<p>Aligning with these global clinical needs, Samsung Medison showcased the HERA Z Series at the 34th International Society of Ultrasound in Obstetrics and Gynecology (ISUOG) World Congress held in London from September 4 to 7.</p>



<p>Under the banner “HERA Z, The Flagship Series,” Samsung Medison presented its vision for next-generation diagnostic solutions, showcasing the series as a unified platform that spans the full spectrum of women’s healthcare.</p>



<p>The HERA Z Series serves as a comprehensive diagnostic platform designed to support maternal and fetal care alike, featuring AI-assisted automated measurement tools and dedicated neonatal probes.</p>



<p></p>



<h2 class="wp-block-heading"><strong>Advanced AI Workflows and Next-Generation Imaging Architecture</strong></h2>



<figure class="wp-block-image size-full"><img loading="lazy" width="1000" height="686" src="https://img.global.news.samsung.com/global/wp-content/uploads/2026/09/07083053/Samsung-More-Products-Samsung-Medison-HERA-Z10_Main2.jpg" alt="An image of HERA Z10" class="wp-image-177117" /></figure>



<p>The newly launched HERA Z10 inherits the proven AI-based workflow solutions of the flagship HERA Z20 while incorporating advanced beamforming technology designed to maximize image clarity. This expanded lineup empowers healthcare institutions to choose from a more versatile selection of premium systems within the HERA Z Series, perfectly tailored to their unique clinical environments and operational goals.</p>



<p>Engineered specifically to streamline clinical workflows through artificial intelligence, the HERA Z10 delivers significant efficiency gains:</p>



<ul class="wp-block-list">
<li><strong>Live ViewAssist<img src="https://s.w.org/images/core/emoji/16.0.1/72x72/2122.png" alt="™" class="wp-smiley" style="height: 1em; max-height: 1em;" /></strong>: According to joint clinical research conducted with Professor Giuseppe Rizzo and Professor Daniele Di Mascio of Sapienza University of Rome — founding members of ISUOG — Live ViewAssist<img src="https://s.w.org/images/core/emoji/16.0.1/72x72/2122.png" alt="™" class="wp-smiley" style="height: 1em; max-height: 1em;" /> reduces scan times during mid-trimester detailed ultrasound examinations by up to 52%.</li>



<li><strong>EzStructure<img src="https://s.w.org/images/core/emoji/16.0.1/72x72/2122.png" alt="™" class="wp-smiley" style="height: 1em; max-height: 1em;" /></strong>: Reduces manual manipulation for key measurement parameters by up to 71%, significantly enhancing measurement consistency and objectivity while dramatically lowering clinician fatigue.</li>
</ul>



<p></p>



<h2 class="wp-block-heading"><strong>Enhanced AI Diagnostic Assistance for High-Risk Pregnancies</strong></h2>



<p>The system also introduces enhanced AI-assisted diagnostic capabilities for complex clinical scenarios:</p>



<ul class="wp-block-list">
<li><strong>MV-Flow 3D<img src="https://s.w.org/images/core/emoji/16.0.1/72x72/2122.png" alt="™" class="wp-smiley" style="height: 1em; max-height: 1em;" /></strong>: Captures microvascular blood flow at high resolution, providing critical clinical value for managing high-risk pregnancies, aiding in the diagnosis of placenta accreta and evaluation of  abnormal uterine or umbilical cord blood vessels.</li>



<li><strong>FibroidVue<img src="https://s.w.org/images/core/emoji/16.0.1/72x72/2122.png" alt="™" class="wp-smiley" style="height: 1em; max-height: 1em;" /></strong>: Utilizes AI to automatically segment, visualize, and measure the uterus, endometrium, and uterine fibroids, facilitating intuitive and efficient clinical assessments.</li>
</ul>

We believe […] <figure class="wp-block-image size-full"><img loading="lazy" width="1000" height="563" src="https://img.global.news.samsung.com/global/wp-content/uploads/2026/09/04103543/Samsung-Digital-Appliances-Long-Term-Value-Software-Updates_main1.jpg" alt="Samsung Electronics today announced major software updates for select refrigerators and laundry appliances." class="wp-image-177098" /></figure>



<p>Samsung Electronics today announced major software updates<sup data-fn="bce13094-4ddc-4d86-9090-398d454c911f" class="fn"><a href="#bce13094-4ddc-4d86-9090-398d454c911f" id="bce13094-4ddc-4d86-9090-398d454c911f-link">1</a></sup> for select refrigerators and laundry appliances. Beginning in September, the update to Tizen OS 10.0 will bring key services and features to select existing appliances in line with those offered on models launched this year.</p>



<p>“Home appliances are products that consumers rely on for many years. We believe supporting them over time requires not only reliable hardware, but also software that continues to evolve,” said Jeong Seung Moon, Executive Vice President and Head of the R&D Team of the Digital Appliances (DA) Business at Samsung Electronics. “Through this latest regular software update, we aim to help our appliances remain useful and up to date throughout the ownership journey, enhancing the long-term value consumers receive from products they already own.”</p>



<p></p>



<h2 class="wp-block-heading"><strong>Bespoke AI Refrigerator Family Hub’s Newest AI Features Expand Globally</strong></h2>



<p>Building on the software update first introduced to <a href="https://bit.ly/3QTaNiD" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer noopener">select Bespoke AI Refrigerator Family Hubs in the U.S. in May</a>, Samsung is expanding its newest AI-powered refrigerator experiences to additional global markets.</p>



<p>Refrigerators with built-in cameras and AI Vision, including 21.5-inch and 32-inch Family Hub released between 2021 and 2025 — as well as select 9-inch Family Hub models released in 2025 — will receive the updated AI Vision built with Google Gemini.<sup data-fn="e6e7c5ab-0ff9-4220-860c-ec34d7be2070" class="fn"><a href="#e6e7c5ab-0ff9-4220-860c-ec34d7be2070" id="e6e7c5ab-0ff9-4220-860c-ec34d7be2070-link">2</a></sup> The update expands the refrigerator’s ability to recognize and manage food items, helping users keep track of what is stored inside and make everyday food management easier.</p>



<p>With this update, the refrigerator can recognize a much wider range of fresh and packaged foods, including branded and region-specific items.</p>



<p>In addition, screen-based experiences like Now Brief<sup data-fn="adc3d7b3-1c88-435d-baf4-001513a47f88" class="fn"><a href="#adc3d7b3-1c88-435d-baf4-001513a47f88" id="adc3d7b3-1c88-435d-baf4-001513a47f88-link">3</a></sup> and What’s for Today? provide users with personalized information and help simplify meal decisions on a daily basis.</p>



<p></p>



<h2 class="wp-block-heading"><strong>Seamless Interaction With Bespoke AI Laundry Appliances</strong></h2>



<p>Samsung is also extending its latest software experiences to select laundry appliances released in 2024 and 2025, including Bespoke AI Laundry Combo, Washer, and Dryer models.</p>



<p>A key part of the update is Bixby,<sup data-fn="64eac909-2a04-4a0c-82a9-0c04e33a9d15" class="fn"><a href="#64eac909-2a04-4a0c-82a9-0c04e33a9d15" id="64eac909-2a04-4a0c-82a9-0c04e33a9d15-link">4</a></sup> which enhances voice recognition and enables more natural interactions with laundry appliances. This allows users to communicate with their appliances more intuitively while carrying out everyday laundry tasks. For example, users can simply say, “When will my laundry be finished?” and Bixby will understand and reply with a phrase such as, “Your laundry is set to finish in two hours and 25 minutes.”<sup data-fn="d74f5250-5a94-48f9-a57c-92542a1a63b7" class="fn"><a href="#d74f5250-5a94-48f9-a57c-92542a1a63b7" id="d74f5250-5a94-48f9-a57c-92542a1a63b7-link">5</a></sup></p>



<p>The update will introduce a refreshed interface aligned with Samsung’s latest laundry experience, offering more intuitive controls and information delivery on applicable models equipped with 7-inch Smart Screens. This will be achieved by adding Easy Mode,<sup data-fn="753a2ef0-9021-4629-91fd-e688cbf30465" class="fn"><a href="#753a2ef0-9021-4629-91fd-e688cbf30465" id="753a2ef0-9021-4629-91fd-e688cbf30465-link">6</a></sup> which streamlines the screen layout and simplifies key information in an easy-to-access format.</p>



<p></p>

Actual timing, supported features, and availability may vary by product, model year, and market.<sup data-fn="beafa76f-fd77-4f18-8118-200411d59b78" class="fn"><a href="#beafa76f-fd77-4f18-8118-200411d59b78" id="beafa76f-fd77-4f18-8118-200411d59b78-link">7</a></sup></p>

<p>“Samsung looks beyond performance and functionality to consider how appliances fit naturally into people’s living spaces and deliver meaningful experiences in everyday use,” said Hyoung Min Park, Vice President and Head of the Customer Experience Team of the Digital Appliances (DA) Business at Samsung Electronics. “We will continue to combine AI with differentiated design and engineering to deliver greater convenience and efficiency that consumers can experience in their daily lives.”</p>

<h2 class="wp-block-heading"><strong>Bespoke AI WindFree Pro Air Conditioner Wins in Best in Design</strong></h2>



<figure class="wp-block-image size-full"><img loading="lazy" width="1000" height="445" src="https://img.global.news.samsung.com/global/wp-content/uploads/2026/09/03114700/Samsung-Digital-Appliances-IFA-2026-IFA-Innovation-Awards-2026-Bespoke-AI-WindFree-Pro-and-Refrigerators_main1_F.jpg" alt="Bespoke AI WindFree Pro Air Conditioner" class="wp-image-177052" /></figure>



<p>The Bespoke AI WindFree Pro Air Conditioner<sup data-fn="b90dd2ba-e3cf-45f2-a7dd-a9664e304d17" class="fn"><a href="#b90dd2ba-e3cf-45f2-a7dd-a9664e304d17" id="b90dd2ba-e3cf-45f2-a7dd-a9664e304d17-link">1</a></sup> was named a winner in the Best in Design category, thanks to a Grid Design that harmonizes with the vertical and horizontal lines as well as the repetitive grids and planes commonly found in modern interiors. Its streamlined form reduces visually prominent functional elements, allowing the air conditioner to integrate naturally into the surrounding space.</p>



<p>As an honoree in the Best in Home Appliances category, the product also offers AI-powered technologies designed to provide personalized comfort. Three Motion Wings enable a variety of airflow patterns,<sup data-fn="db47e7f1-b044-446d-85bf-05e4608ad8d8" class="fn"><a href="#db47e7f1-b044-446d-85bf-05e4608ad8d8" id="db47e7f1-b044-446d-85bf-05e4608ad8d8-link">2</a></sup> while AI Motion Wind uses a radar sensor to adapt airflow based on users’ preferences and movements.<sup data-fn="1505f8b6-a031-46ff-8223-0b4010c7709d" class="fn"><a href="#1505f8b6-a031-46ff-8223-0b4010c7709d" id="1505f8b6-a031-46ff-8223-0b4010c7709d-link">3</a></sup> WindFree<img src="https://s.w.org/images/core/emoji/16.0.1/72x72/2122.png" alt="™" class="wp-smiley" style="height: 1em; max-height: 1em;" /> Wearable Good Sleep connects to a compatible wearable device<sup data-fn="fdd2e5d7-1221-4efb-867c-ed2a8293537b" class="fn"><a href="#fdd2e5d7-1221-4efb-867c-ed2a8293537b" id="fdd2e5d7-1221-4efb-867c-ed2a8293537b-link">4</a></sup> and syncs with the user’s body signals to automatically adjust cooling based on the user’s sleep state.</p>



<p></p>



<h2 class="wp-block-heading"><strong>Highlighted IFA Innovation Awards 2026 Honorees</strong></h2>



<p></p>



<h4 class="wp-block-heading has-medium-font-size"><strong>Bespoke AI Refrigerator Family Hub</strong></h4>



<figure class="wp-block-image size-full"><img loading="lazy" width="1000" height="667" src="https://img.global.news.samsung.com/global/wp-content/uploads/2026/09/03113811/Samsung-Digital-Appliances-IFA-2026-IFA-Innovation-Awards-2026-Bespoke-AI-WindFree-Pro-and-Refrigerators_main2.jpg" alt="Bespoke AI Refrigerator Family Hub" class="wp-image-177047" /></figure>



<p>The Bespoke AI Refrigerator Family Hub<sup data-fn="362ce1f8-41fa-4f47-9098-307572b21928" class="fn"><a href="#362ce1f8-41fa-4f47-9098-307572b21928" id="362ce1f8-41fa-4f47-9098-307572b21928-link">5</a></sup> was named an honoree in the Best in Design category. It offers a personalized experience for consumers with its 32-inch display, as well as food management and connected kitchen capabilities.<sup data-fn="794aa5cf-b844-4464-bf49-6775d85abb60" class="fn"><a href="#794aa5cf-b844-4464-bf49-6775d85abb60" id="794aa5cf-b844-4464-bf49-6775d85abb60-link">6</a></sup></p>



<p>AI Vision uses an internal camera<sup data-fn="276dc6e5-c6e3-4fb7-9cba-47750d7edce0" class="fn"><a href="#276dc6e5-c6e3-4fb7-9cba-47750d7edce0" id="276dc6e5-c6e3-4fb7-9cba-47750d7edce0-link">7</a></sup> to recognize food items as they are placed in or removed from the refrigerator.<sup data-fn="149dc6b7-48d7-4ce0-b152-11bee01a2358" class="fn"><a href="#149dc6b7-48d7-4ce0-b152-11bee01a2358" id="149dc6b7-48d7-4ce0-b152-11bee01a2358-link">8</a></sup> With this feature, users can track the use-by-dates of food items<sup data-fn="57ab899a-78e9-4254-936f-5b2113600f8f" class="fn"><a href="#57ab899a-78e9-4254-936f-5b2113600f8f" id="57ab899a-78e9-4254-936f-5b2113600f8f-link">9</a></sup> and receive recipe recommendations from the SmartThings Food app. Additionally, Auto Open Door enables hands-free access through voice commands or a simple touch,<sup data-fn="1455cd95-1027-4073-b18f-da03ca23a23a" class="fn"><a href="#1455cd95-1027-4073-b18f-da03ca23a23a" id="1455cd95-1027-4073-b18f-da03ca23a23a-link">10</a></sup> allowing users to open and close the refrigerator door when their hands are full.</p>



<p></p>



<h4 class="wp-block-heading has-medium-font-size"><strong>Bespoke AI Refrigerator</strong></h4>



<figure class="wp-block-image size-full"><img loading="lazy" width="1000" height="667" src="https://img.global.news.samsung.com/global/wp-content/uploads/2026/09/03114923/Samsung-Digital-Appliances-IFA-2026-IFA-Innovation-Awards-2026-Bespoke-AI-WindFree-Pro-and-Refrigerators_main3.jpg" alt="Bespoke AI Refrigerator" class="wp-image-177054" /></figure>



<p>The Bespoke AI Refrigerator was named an honoree in the Best in Home Appliances category.</p>



<p>The refrigerator can be installed with gaps of only 4 mm on each side and 20 mm at the top, allowing it to fit seamlessly into existing cabinetry.<sup data-fn="90e7cb84-4c6a-4dbe-a74c-d93563156dfb" class="fn"><a href="#90e7cb84-4c6a-4dbe-a74c-d93563156dfb" id="90e7cb84-4c6a-4dbe-a74c-d93563156dfb-link">11</a></sup> Its zero clearance hinge enables the doors to open fully<sup data-fn="bb97db53-e622-48a7-9e53-7148dcb86186" class="fn"><a href="#bb97db53-e622-48a7-9e53-7148dcb86186" id="bb97db53-e622-48a7-9e53-7148dcb86186-link">12</a></sup> even in tight spaces, providing convenient access to drawers and food items. Thinner doors<sup data-fn="03a8207d-4a22-42b6-910a-be5943a84e07" class="fn"><a href="#03a8207d-4a22-42b6-910a-be5943a84e07" id="03a8207d-4a22-42b6-910a-be5943a84e07-link">13</a></sup> and SpaceMax<img src="https://s.w.org/images/core/emoji/16.0.1/72x72/2122.png" alt="™" class="wp-smiley" style="height: 1em; max-height: 1em;" /> technology also provide more interior space<sup data-fn="02a1e032-d250-4f47-9d28-050c404b18ac" class="fn"><a href="#02a1e032-d250-4f47-9d28-050c404b18ac" id="02a1e032-d250-4f47-9d28-050c404b18ac-link">14</a></sup> without increasing the refrigerator’s external dimensions. Additionally, the product’s AI Hybrid Cooling technology combines a compressor and Peltier element<sup data-fn="306e8892-e707-487e-9b8b-1c2fd2599563" class="fn"><a href="#306e8892-e707-487e-9b8b-1c2fd2599563" id="306e8892-e707-487e-9b8b-1c2fd2599563-link">15</a></sup> to enhance cooling efficiency.</p>



<hr class="wp-block-separator has-alpha-channel-opacity" />



<h4 class="wp-block-heading"><strong>About the IFA Innovation Awards</strong></h4>



<h4 class="wp-block-heading">The IFA Innovation Awards recognize innovative technologies and products across the industry. IFA 2026, the world’s largest event for home and consumer technology, takes place in Berlin, Germany, from September 4-8. </h4>



<p></p>



<h4 class="wp-block-heading">Official: Launched in 2025, the IFA Innovation Awards, presented in partnership with Gadgety Awards, recognize technical excellence across the global technology landscape. The program is open to companies of all sizes worldwide.</h4>

At the Samsung booth, the company paired its latest products with games, activities, and partner experiences designed to support a variety of genres and play styles. Reflecting Gamescom 2026’s “The Heart of […] <p>Samsung Electronics returned to Gamescom 2026 in Cologne, Germany, where players, streamers, media, and industry partners gathered to experience the latest in gaming. At the Samsung booth, the company paired its latest products with games, activities, and partner experiences designed to support a variety of genres and play styles.</p>



<p>Reflecting Gamescom 2026’s “The Heart of Gaming” theme, the booth brought these experiences together across Samsung’s latest displays and gaming technologies. On August 27, Samsung introduced its 2027 Odyssey lineup at its central event, “#PlaySamsung: The Next Era of Gaming Displays,” alongside new developments spanning gaming displays, HDR, and game content.</p>



<p>The audience’s excitement for the new Odyssey gaming monitors’ advanced display technology demonstrated the gaming community’s appetite for innovation tailored to every playstyle.</p>



<figure class="wp-block-image size-full"><img loading="lazy" width="1000" height="667" src="https://img.global.news.samsung.com/global/wp-content/uploads/2026/09/03145015/Samsung-TVs-and-Displays-Odyssey-Gamescom-2026_Main1.jpg" alt="Visitors gather at Samsung booth at Gamescom 2026 in Cologne, Germany." class="wp-image-177061" /><figcaption class="wp-element-caption">▲ Visitors gather at Samsung booth at Gamescom 2026 in Cologne, Germany.</figcaption></figure>



<p></p>



<h2 class="wp-block-heading"><strong>A New Generation of Odyssey Takes the Stage</strong></h2>



<p>At the heart of the booth was Samsung’s newest Odyssey lineup, with the Odyssey OLED G9 and Odyssey OLED G7 making their debut on stage during “#PlaySamsung: The Next Era of Gaming Displays.” The Odyssey OLED G8 and Odyssey G6 were also showcased through digital demonstrations, highlighting the range of screen formats and performance options coming to Samsung’s 2027 lineup.</p>



<p>Popular Twitch streamer Esfand (EsfandTV) joined the stage session to discuss how screen format can change the way players experience and follow action in a game.</p>



<p>“The clarity and contrast of Samsung’s new Odyssey lineup are so nice. Once you see the new monitors, you really notice the difference in how they fully immerse you in the game,” said Esfand.</p>



<figure class="wp-block-image size-full"><img loading="lazy" width="1000" height="329" src="https://img.global.news.samsung.com/global/wp-content/uploads/2026/09/03145630/Samsung-TVs-and-Displays-Odyssey-Gamescom-2026_Main2.jpg" alt="Twitch streamer Esfand joins Samsung on stage at Gamescom 2026 to discuss the role of displays in gaming." class="wp-image-177062" /><figcaption class="wp-element-caption">▲ Twitch streamer Esfand joins Samsung on stage at Gamescom 2026 to discuss the role of displays in gaming.</figcaption></figure>



<p></p>



<h2 class="wp-block-heading"><strong>Seeing More of the Action With Odyssey OLED G9</strong></h2>



<p>The <a href="https://news.samsung.com/global/samsung-introduces-new-odyssey-lineup-for-fast-paced-gaming-at-gamescom-2026#:~:text=Odyssey%20OLED%20G9%3A%2049,want%20even%20faster%20performance." target="_blank" rel="noreferrer noopener"><strong>Odyssey OLED G9 (G95SJ)</strong></a> is the world’s first 49-inch Dual QHD gaming monitor with a 360Hz refresh rate, giving players a broad view of the action across its ultra-wide screen.</p>



<p>At Gamescom, the expansive format put game environments, track layouts, maps, and activity at the edges of the screen into view, demonstrating how a wider display can change the way players follow fast-moving gameplay.</p>



<p></p>



<h2 class="wp-block-heading"><strong>Pulling Worlds Closer With Odyssey OLED G7</strong></h2>



<p>Samsung also unveiled the <a href="https://news.samsung.com/global/samsung-introduces-new-odyssey-lineup-for-fast-paced-gaming-at-gamescom-2026#:~:text=Odyssey%20OLED%20G7%3A%20A,for%20clearer%20daytime%20play." target="_blank" rel="noreferrer noopener"><strong>Odyssey OLED G7 (G75SJ)</strong></a>, the world’s first 42-inch 16:9 curved OLED gaming monitor.</p>



<p>With approximately 72% more screen area than a 32-inch model, the display gives games with detailed environments more room to unfold. Its 1000R curve brings the edges of the screen closer to the player’s line of sight, creating a format suited to games where maps, interfaces, and action extend across the display.</p>



<p>“The scale and curve of the new Odyssey OLED G7 create a whole new level of immersion. It’s great that Samsung designed the monitor with open-world environments in mind,” said a #PlaySamsung booth visitor.</p>



<p></p>



<h2 class="wp-block-heading"><strong>More Options for Competitive and Multi-Mode Gaming</strong></h2>



<p>The new Odyssey lineup extends beyond large OLED formats, giving players additional options based on how they play.</p>



<p>The 32-inch <a href="https://news.samsung.com/global/samsung-introduces-new-odyssey-lineup-for-fast-paced-gaming-at-gamescom-2026#:~:text=Odyssey%20OLED%20G8%3A%204K,ports%2C%20and%20KVM%20support." target="_blank" rel="noreferrer noopener"><strong>Odyssey OLED G8 (G80SJ)</strong></a> gives players three display modes to choose from. Triple Mode supports UHD at up to 360Hz, QHD at up to 520Hz, and FHD at up to 680Hz.</p>



<p>The 27-inch <a href="https://news.samsung.com/global/samsung-introduces-new-odyssey-lineup-for-fast-paced-gaming-at-gamescom-2026#:~:text=Odyssey%20G6%3A%20Pushing,half%20of%202026." target="_blank" rel="noreferrer noopener"><strong>Odyssey G6 (G60H)</strong></a> is the world’s first 1,100Hz gaming monitor. Its Dual Mode lets players choose between QHD at up to 600Hz for sharper detail or HD at up to 1,100Hz for the monitor’s highest refresh-rate setting.</p>



<p>“I’m excited about Samsung’s emphasis on frame rate. These monitors will be a game changer for competitive gaming,” said a #PlaySamsung booth visitor.</p>



<figure class="wp-block-image size-full"><img loading="lazy" width="1000" height="667" src="https://img.global.news.samsung.com/global/wp-content/uploads/2026/09/03151052/Samsung-TVs-and-Displays-Odyssey-Gamescom-2026_Main3.jpg" alt="Samsung’s 2027 Odyssey lineup offers a range of screen formats and performance options for different gaming styles." class="wp-image-177065" /><figcaption class="wp-element-caption">▲ Samsung’s 2027 Odyssey lineup offers a range of screen formats and performance options for different gaming styles.</figcaption></figure>



<p>Together, the four new models show how Samsung’s Odyssey lineup is expanding across ultra-wide, curved, high-refresh-rate, and multi-mode formats, giving players more ways to match their display to the games they play.</p>



<p></p>



<h2 class="wp-block-heading"><strong>HDR10+ GAMING Connects Game Development and Display Technology</strong></h2>



<p>During the stage session, Samsung shared plans to expand <strong>HDR10+ GAMING</strong> support as game developers look for more consistent HDR presentation across compatible displays. As part of this effort, Samsung is working with Activision Blizzard to bring HDR10+ GAMING to its upcoming title, <strong>Call of Duty<sup>®</sup>: Modern Warfare<sup>®</sup> 4</strong>.</p>



<p>Samsung plans to expand HDR10+ GAMING support to more than 20 AAA titles by the first half of 2027. The technology can support brighter highlights, deeper dark scenes, and accurate contrast across different game environments, while reducing the need for players to manually adjust HDR settings before starting a compatible game.</p>



<p>“There’s an entire ecosystem in gaming, from the developer to the hardware companies. Whenever these companies come together, the product is always going to be the best thing players can get,” said Esfand.</p>



<figure class="wp-block-image size-full"><img loading="lazy" width="1000" height="329" src="https://img.global.news.samsung.com/global/wp-content/uploads/2026/09/03151446/Samsung-TVs-and-Displays-Odyssey-Gamescom-2026_Main4.jpg" alt="Samsung highlights the future of HDR10+ GAMING during “The Next Era of Gaming Displays” stage session at Gamescom 2026." class="wp-image-177066" /><figcaption class="wp-element-caption">▲ Samsung highlights the future of HDR10+ GAMING during “The Next Era of Gaming Displays” stage session at Gamescom 2026.</figcaption></figure>



<p>“Developers want their games to look consistent across different scenes and displays, without requiring players to adjust calibration settings. HDR10+ GAMING gives developers another way to preserve their HDR presentation while helping players see more detail across a range of environments,” said Pepe Dijkstra, Director of the European Visual Organization at Samsung Electronics.</p>



<figure class="wp-block-image size-full"><img loading="lazy" width="1000" height="667" src="https://img.global.news.samsung.com/global/wp-content/uploads/2026/09/03151615/Samsung-TVs-and-Displays-Odyssey-Gamescom-2026_Main5.jpg" alt="Samsung Odyssey’s HDR 10+ GAMING support allows Call of Duty®: Modern Warfare® 4 players to play the game in vivid graphics as intented by developers." class="wp-image-177067" /><figcaption class="wp-element-caption">▲ Samsung Odyssey’s HDR 10+ GAMING support allows Call of Duty<sup>®</sup>: Modern Warfare<sup>®</sup> 4 players to play the game in vivid graphics as intended by developers.</figcaption></figure>



<p>The action continued on August 29 at the #PlaySamsung booth with the <strong>Samsung Odyssey Showdown: Call of Duty<sup>®</sup>: Modern Warfare<sup>®</sup> 4</strong>. The 6v6 combat match with gaming content creators Zyro, Rewinside, EloTRiX, and Rosemondy gave visitors an exciting opportunity to play the title ahead of its official October 23 release.</p>



<figure class="wp-block-image size-full"><img loading="lazy" width="1000" height="750" src="https://img.global.news.samsung.com/global/wp-content/uploads/2026/09/03151734/Samsung-TVs-and-Displays-Odyssey-Gamescom-2026_Main6.jpg" alt="Visitors and creators go head-to-head during the Samsung Odyssey Showdown: Call of Duty®: Modern Warfare® 4 match at the #PlaySamsung booth." class="wp-image-177068" /><figcaption class="wp-element-caption">▲ Visitors and creators go head-to-head during the Samsung Odyssey Showdown: Call of Duty<sup>®</sup>: Modern Warfare<sup>®</sup> 4 match at the #PlaySamsung booth.</figcaption></figure>



<p>The experience showed how compatible displays can use scene-by-scene lighting and color information from the game to present its visuals. A dedicated Call of Duty<sup>® </sup>photo zone recreated scenery and props from the game, giving visitors another way to step into its world.</p>



<figure class="wp-block-image size-full"><img loading="lazy" width="1000" height="667" src="https://img.global.news.samsung.com/global/wp-content/uploads/2026/09/03152005/Samsung-TVs-and-Displays-Odyssey-Gamescom-2026_Main7.jpg" alt="Visitors take on Call of Duty®: Modern Warfare® 4 at Samsung’s #PlaySamsung booth." class="wp-image-177069" /><figcaption class="wp-element-caption">▲ Visitors take on Call of Duty<sup>®</sup>: Modern Warfare<sup>®</sup> 4 at Samsung’s #PlaySamsung booth.</figcaption></figure>



<p></p>



<h2 class="wp-block-heading"><strong>From Realistic Adventures to Glasses-Free 3D</strong></h2>



<p>The gaming experiences continued across the rest of the booth, where Samsung connected its displays with titles spanning open-world adventures, glasses-free 3D, and TV gaming.</p>



<figure class="wp-block-image size-full"><img loading="lazy" width="1000" height="667" src="https://img.global.news.samsung.com/global/wp-content/uploads/2026/09/03152232/Samsung-TVs-and-Displays-Odyssey-Gamescom-2026_Main8.jpg" alt=" Rick van Beem, Senior Director of Marketing and Public Relations at Pearl Abyss Europe, showcases how the Odyssey G8 enhances Crimson Desert gameplay." class="wp-image-177070" /><figcaption class="wp-element-caption">▲ Rick van Beem, Senior Director of Marketing and Public Relations at Pearl Abyss Europe, showcases how the Odyssey G8 enhances Crimson Desert gameplay.</figcaption></figure>



<p>At <strong>Pearl Abyss</strong>, visitors got a closer look at Crimson Desert Enhanced, a new version of the open-world action-adventure featuring new cutscenes and dialogue that strengthen the story’s narrative flow. During the session, Rick van Beem, Senior Director of Marketing and Public Relations at Pearl Abyss Europe, highlighted how the <strong>Odyssey G8</strong> brings the game’s environment to life with features including Dual Mode and HDR10+ GAMING support.</p>



<p>The experience continued in a dedicated console area, where Crimson Desert was paired with Samsung’s 83-inch <strong>OLED TV </strong>S99H, named Best Gaming TV of 2026 by T3. The TV features a 165Hz refresh rate, Game Mode, and Ultimate Gaming Pack. Visitors could also take part in Crimson Desert quizzes and activities inspired by elements of the game for a chance to receive exclusive merchandise.</p>



<figure class="wp-block-image size-full"><img loading="lazy" width="1000" height="667" src="https://img.global.news.samsung.com/global/wp-content/uploads/2026/09/03152728/Samsung-TVs-and-Displays-Odyssey-Gamescom-2026_Main9.jpg" alt="Visitors experience Crimson Desert on Samsung’s 83-inch OLED TV S99H." class="wp-image-177071" /><figcaption class="wp-element-caption">▲ Visitors experience Crimson Desert on Samsung’s 83-inch OLED TV S99H.</figcaption></figure>



<p>Meanwhile, newly supported titles, <strong>Cyberpunk 2077</strong> and <strong>Cronos: The New Dawn</strong>, were featured on stage and at Samsung’s dedicated Odyssey 3D zone to demonstrate how Odyssey 3D can create depth and spatial perspective across detailed game worlds.</p>



<p>The glasses-free experience uses Odyssey 3D display technology to create depth and spatial perspective within the game, allowing visitors to experience the worlds from a different perspective without specialized glasses.</p>



<figure class="wp-block-image size-full"><img loading="lazy" width="1000" height="667" src="https://img.global.news.samsung.com/global/wp-content/uploads/2026/09/03152930/Samsung-TVs-and-Displays-Odyssey-Gamescom-2026_Main10.jpg" alt="Visitors experience glasses-free 3D gameplay on Samsung Odyssey 3D." class="wp-image-177072" /><figcaption class="wp-element-caption">▲ Visitors experience glasses-free 3D gameplay on Samsung Odyssey 3D.</figcaption></figure>



<p>The booth also featured a cloud gaming area invited visitors to play through <strong>Samsung Gaming Hub</strong> on <strong>48-inch OLED TVs</strong>, showing how gaming can be enjoyed directly on a TV.</p>



<figure class="wp-block-image size-full"><img loading="lazy" width="1000" height="667" src="https://img.global.news.samsung.com/global/wp-content/uploads/2026/09/03153352/Samsung-TVs-and-Displays-Odyssey-Gamescom-2026_Main11.jpg" alt="Visitors explore cloud gaming through Samsung Gaming Hub on a Samsung OLED TV." class="wp-image-177073" /><figcaption class="wp-element-caption">▲ Visitors explore cloud gaming through Samsung Gaming Hub on a Samsung OLED TV.</figcaption></figure>



<p>From the queue areas to the gameplay stations and interactive activities, the different experiences connected the booth into one broader look at how players can engage with games across competitive shooters, open-world adventures, console gaming, cloud gaming, and glasses-free 3D.</p>



<p></p>



<h2 class="wp-block-heading"><strong>More Choice Across Speed, Screen Size, and OLED Picture Quality</strong></h2>



<p>At Gamescom 2026, Samsung showed how gaming displays are evolving alongside the games and players they serve.</p>



<p>The 2027 Odyssey lineup gives players more choice across screen formats, performance settings and OLED picture quality — from the expansive Odyssey OLED G9 and curved OLED G7 to the high-refresh-rate Odyssey G6 and multi-mode Odyssey OLED G8.</p>



<p>Across the booth, partnerships and gaming experiences put those technologies into context, showing how Samsung displays can support different genres, platforms, and ways of playing.</p>



<p>Samsung has led the global gaming monitor market for seven consecutive years, continuing to expand its full-stack portfolio as gaming evolves across screens, formats, and communities.</p> Meet the Generation17 Young Leaders: The Story of Soumya Dabriwal https://news.samsung.com/global/meet-the-generation17-young-leaders-the-story-of-soumya-dabriwal Fri, 04 Sep 2026 08:00:00 +0000 Samsung Newsroom Mobile Generation17 Global Goals UNDP Young Leaders https://bit.ly/4iqvXk7 Soumya Dabriwal is a member of Generation17, a Samsung and United Nations Development Programme (UNDP) partnership empowering young people who are contributing to the Global Goals. Since 2020, the initiative has supported Young Leaders worldwide with Samsung Galaxy technology, mentorship, and networking opportunities to amplify their stories and solutions. Soumya Dabriwal was 21, volunteering as […] <p>Soumya Dabriwal is a member of <a href="https://www.undp.org/generation17" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer noopener">Generation17</a>, a Samsung and <a href="https://www.undp.org/" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer noopener">United Nations Development Programme (UNDP)</a> partnership empowering young people who are contributing to the <a href="https://sdgs.un.org/goals" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer noopener">Global Goals</a>.</p>



<p>Since 2020, the initiative has supported Young Leaders worldwide with Samsung Galaxy technology, mentorship, and networking opportunities to amplify their stories and solutions.</p>



<p>Soumya Dabriwal was 21, volunteering as a math teacher in villages in India and Ghana, when she noticed a pattern she couldn’t explain. Girls were absent from class far more often than boys — even siblings from the same household, where the brother would show up while his sister stayed home.</p>



<figure class="wp-block-embed is-type-video is-provider-youtube wp-block-embed-youtube wp-embed-aspect-16-9 wp-has-aspect-ratio"><div class="wp-block-embed__wrapper">
<iframe loading="lazy" title="Meet the Generation17 Young Leaders: The Story of Soumya Dabriwal | Samsung" width="500" height="281" src="https://www.youtube.com/embed/UAgKjWnCm84?feature=oembed" frameborder="0" allow="accelerometer; autoplay; clipboard-write; encrypted-media; gyroscope; picture-in-picture; web-share" referrerpolicy="strict-origin-when-cross-origin" allowfullscreen></iframe> 
</div></figure>



<p>When Soumya asked, no one would answer why. The reality hit her when students warned her not to drink water in the heat because the school had no toilets. It quickly became clear: without basic facilities, girls had no choice but to skip school during their periods.</p>



<p>She began researching and found that around the world, girls lacked <a href="https://www.who.int/news/item/28-05-2024-global-report-reveals-major-gaps-in-menstrual-health-and-hygiene-in-schools" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer noopener">health education, support, and resources</a> to stay in school and achieve their dreams. “I was extremely frustrated and disappointed. It felt unfair that biology was restricting girls from attaining their full potential,” Soumya says. “Instead of waiting for someone else to take action, I became obsessed with wanting to solve the problem.”</p>



<p></p>



<h2 class="wp-block-heading"><strong>Tech-Enabled, Women-Led</strong></h2>



<p>In 2017, she co-founded Project <a href="https://baala.in/" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer noopener">Baala</a>, which means “young girl” in Hindi, a social enterprise addressing menstrual hygiene and reproductive health access through innovative solutions. The mission is to advance the UN Global Goals for quality education (<a href="https://globalgoals.org/goals/4-quality-education/" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer noopener">Goal 4</a>), good health and well-being (<a href="https://globalgoals.org/goals/3-good-health-and-well-being/" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer noopener">Goal 3</a>), gender equality (<a href="https://globalgoals.org/goals/5-gender-equality/" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer noopener">Goal 5</a>), and decent work and economic growth (<a href="https://globalgoals.org/goals/8-decent-work-and-economic-growth/" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer noopener">Goal 8</a>).</p>



<figure class="wp-block-image size-full"><img loading="lazy" width="1000" height="563" src="https://img.global.news.samsung.com/global/wp-content/uploads/2026/09/02143826/Samsung-Mobile-Generation17-Global-Goals-Soumya-Dabriwal_Main1.jpg" alt="Community workshops are at the heart of Baala's approach, combining education with open conversations that help break long-standing taboos." class="wp-image-177002" /><figcaption class="wp-element-caption">▲ Community workshops are at the heart of Baala’s approach, combining education with open conversations that help break long-standing taboos.</figcaption></figure>



<p>Soumya began leading workshops where girls learned about their bodies and health. But education alone wasn’t enough. Without access to affordable, sustainable feminine hygiene products, girls were still missing school. Since nothing on the market met their needs, Soumya created a prototype using materials she found at home, turning a simple idea into a solution that today reaches millions of girls.</p>



<p>Next, she and her growing team began training local women to deliver health education and feminine hygiene products. Known as Baala Bosses, these women earn an income, often for the first time, while helping support their families and gaining confidence as community leaders.</p>



<p>Technology has played a critical role in Baala’s growth. Social media connects the organization with women and girls around the world, while online training enables Baala Bosses to learn and share knowledge wherever they are. Digital field data helps the team measure its impact and continuously strengthen its programs. “Technology has really helped us scale and reach people we wouldn’t have been able to otherwise,” says Soumya.</p>



<p>Over the past eight years, Baala has trained more than 4,500 Baala Bosses, reached 1.8 million women and girls, engaged more than 10,000 men to help break the stigma around women’s health and distributed 5.1 million reusable period products through more than 260 partnerships with grassroots organizations.</p>



<figure class="wp-block-image size-full"><img loading="lazy" width="1000" height="563" src="https://img.global.news.samsung.com/global/wp-content/uploads/2026/09/02144054/Samsung-Mobile-Generation17-Global-Goals-Soumya-Dabriwal_Main2.jpg" alt="Soumya and the Baala team meet at their Delhi office to review insights from the field and plan future initiatives." class="wp-image-177003" /><figcaption class="wp-element-caption">▲ Soumya and the Baala team meet at their Delhi office to review insights from the field and plan future initiatives.</figcaption></figure>



<p></p>



<h2 class="wp-block-heading"><strong>Building a Movement for Women’s Health</strong></h2>



<p>Building Baala wasn’t easy. Soumya recalls obstacles at every level, from convincing people to take a young female founder seriously to confronting long-held cultural taboos. Those experiences only reinforced her belief that lasting change had to come from within communities themselves.</p>



<p>Today, Baala Bosses are leading change in their own neighborhoods by educating families, creating opportunities for others and inspiring the next generation. “Women who once kept their faces covered now walk with their hair flying, speaking openly about this topic,” Soumya says. “Some become community leaders. Some become entrepreneurs. Some become health mobilizers. Some become teachers.”</p>



<figure class="wp-block-image size-full"><img loading="lazy" width="1000" height="563" src="https://img.global.news.samsung.com/global/wp-content/uploads/2026/09/02144417/Samsung-Mobile-Generation17-Global-Goals-Soumya-Dabriwal_Main3.jpg" alt="Participants gather for a photo following one of Baala's community workshops in northern India." class="wp-image-177004" /><figcaption class="wp-element-caption">▲ Participants gather for a photo following one of Baala’s community workshops in northern India.</figcaption></figure>



<p></p>



<h2 class="wp-block-heading"><strong>Advancing Women’s Health, Rights, and Opportunities</strong></h2>



<p>Soumya, now 30, dreams of continuing to expand Baala’s impact. By 2030, she hopes to reach 10 million women and girls around the world through product innovation, expanded education, and technology-enabled solutions.</p>



<p>To make that vision a reality, her team launched the Baala Action Lab, an online platform that partners with universities to advance women’s health worldwide. The initiative combines academic research with real-world evidence to inform better policies and investments in women’s health.</p>



<p>Soumya says the ultimate measure of success will be the day Baala is no longer needed — when women and girls have the knowledge, opportunities, and support they need to thrive on their own.</p>



<p>Until then, she keeps showing up.</p>
